### [103] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验，在OpenVLA-OFT中引入可学习的记忆令牌实现跨时间步的循环机制，在部分可观测任务上显著提升成功率（0.42→0.84），且在全观测场景无性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型假设预测仅基于当前观测，在部分可观测环境下会失效。已有记忆增强VLA工作同时引入循环、检索、压缩模块等多种机制，循环机制本身的贡献被复杂架构所掩盖。作者旨在通过控制性研究，明确最小化循环机制的能力边界。

Method: 在预训练VLA骨干网络中引入少量可学习的记忆令牌，这些令牌跨时间步传递并通过自注意力机制更新。采用截断时间反向传播(TBPTT)端到端训练，无需辅助损失函数或架构变更。该方法被实例化为μVLA，包含记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K、记忆更新规则（跨步梯度或分离EMA）三个可调参数，确保循环是唯一变量。

Result: 在MIKASA-Robo基准上，最优配置下五个训练任务平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84；在同构记忆需求的留出任务上成功率达0.23（无记忆基线0.07）。在需要不同记忆结构的任务上性能接近基线。在LIBERO基准上，最强循环变体达到96.2%平均成功率，表明在全观测场景无性能退化。

Conclusion: 最小化的骨干内循环机制在特定场景下足够有效：能解决与训练任务记忆结构相似的部分可观测问题，且不影响全观测场景性能。但当任务需要不同记忆结构时，性能增益有限，表明需要更复杂的记忆架构。该研究校准了最小循环机制的能力包络，明确了其适用与不足的边界。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [104] [Clipping Makes Distributed and Federated Asynchronous SGD Robust to Stragglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13287)
*Samuel Erickson,Mikael Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上解释了梯度裁剪如何通过消除最大延迟依赖来稳定异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）的训练过程，证明了其在次Weibull噪声假设下的期望收敛性和首次高概率收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）中，慢速工作节点导致更新延迟，损害收敛性。尽管经验上观察到梯度裁剪能稳定深度学习模型的异步训练，但缺乏理论解释，这促使作者研究梯度裁剪的收敛性。

Method: 提出了基于梯度裁剪的ASGD理论分析框架。假设梯度噪声服从次Weibull分布（一类包含次高斯和次指数分布的重尾分布），分析了梯度裁剪对异步优化收敛性的影响。

Result: 理论分析证明，梯度裁剪能够消除ASGD的Oracle复杂度对最大延迟的依赖。首次在异步优化中建立了期望收敛性以及高概率收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 梯度裁剪为ASGD提供了理论上的稳定性保证，使其收敛性不再受慢速工作节点延迟的负面影响，从而有效支撑了大规模分布式训练中的异步并行策略。

Abstract: In modern machine learning, parallelization of training is an important strategy for increasing scale. Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD), which maximizes the utilization of available hardware by avoiding waiting for slow workers. However, with constant step sizes, the convergence of ASGD is nonetheless affected negatively by slow workers due to large delays in updates. At the same time, it has been empirically observed in asynchronous training of deep learning models that gradient clipping "stabilizes" training. In this work, we provide a theoretical justification for this behavior, as we show that clipping removes the dependence of the maximum delay in the oracle complexity. We employ a sub-Weibull model of gradient noise which generalizes sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions to more heavy-tailed distributions, motivated by empirical observations in deep learning. We show convergence in expectation, and the first time in asynchronous optimization, convergence with high probability.

</details>


### [105] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比了规则模型和机器学习(ML)模型在交通微观仿真中用于预测事故频率的表现，发现ML模型能产生符合现实数据的冲突预测，而规则模型无法提供有效预测，证明了ML模型在无需特定校准的情况下改善事故预测的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于微观仿真的安全研究采用简化的规则驾驶行为模型，虽然能合理再现交通流，但往往无法生成真实的冲突动态，限制了事故预测准确性。机器学习行为模型的出现为提高微观仿真真实性和事故预测精度提供了新机会。

Method: 在英国利兹市五个真实信号控制交叉口进行交通微观仿真，分别使用标准规则模型和先进的ML模型。采用二维碰撞时间(TTC)指标分析仿真车辆轨迹以识别冲突，然后应用极值理论(EVT)对冲突进行建模以预测事故频率。

Result: ML模型产生的冲突预测结果与现实事故数据一致，而规则模型无法提供有意义的预测（可能缺乏针对特定交叉口的模型校准）。直接使用ML生成的仿真事故预测现实事故频率效果不佳，表明当前ML模型能真实再现冲突但尚不能生成真实的事故。

Conclusion: ML行为模型在改善基于仿真冲突的事故预测方面具有前景，无需进行特定位置的模型校准。研究结果为基于ML的交通微观仿真指明了明确的未来发展方向。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [106] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Conformal QoT框架，通过结合统计保证的QoT估计与运营决策策略，解决了域偏移下的光路可行性预测问题，将预测准确率从92%提升至99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光网络中，QoT估计面临域偏移（domain shift）问题，即训练数据与实际部署环境之间的分布差异导致预测可靠性下降，现有方法难以提供统计意义上的预测保证。

Method: 提出策略驱动的Conformal QoT框架，将保形预测（conformal prediction）统计方法与运营决策策略相结合，实现域偏移条件下具有统计保证的QoT估计。

Result: 在开放数据集上，该方法将光路可行性预测准确率从92%提升至99.6%，同时提供统计可靠性的理论保证。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架有效解决了域偏移下的QoT估计可靠性问题，为光网络运营决策提供了高精度且具有统计保证的预测能力，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [107] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了首个针对海豚通信的大规模自监督学习模型Dolph2Vec，基于五年纵向录音数据训练，在签名哨声分类和检测任务上显著优于通用模型，并能捕捉可解释的声学单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物声学领域的自监督学习模型优先考虑跨物种的广泛泛化能力，而非针对揭示个体通信系统的细粒度结构进行优化。作者旨在开发一种特定物种的大规模SSL模型，以深入分析海豚通信的精细结构。

Method: 1) 收集并发布超过五年的纵向录音数据集，包含五个已知海豚在半自然海洋环境中的录音；2) 将Wav2Vec2.0架构适配到该领域，开发Dolph2Vec模型，这是首个专门在此数据上训练的特定物种SSL模型；3) 在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两个生物学相关任务上对模型进行基准测试。

Result: Dolph2Vec在签名哨声分类和哨声检测两项任务上均显著优于通用基线模型。学习到的嵌入和码本结构能够捕捉可解释的声学单元，这些单元与海豚哨声类别对齐，并可能揭示子哨声结构，从而实现对通信模式的细粒度分析。

Conclusion: 研究表明，自监督学习不仅可以作为性能优越的模型，还能作为科学工具来探索动物通信研究中的假设，为深入理解海豚通信系统提供了新的方法途径。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

</details>


### [108] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPOP通过添加门控惩罚项扩展DPO，利用参考模型自身生成的响应信号，在AlpacaEval 2.0上相比DPO、SimPO和AlphaDPO分别获得5.3%和4.4%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线偏好优化方法（如DPO及其变体）仅使用静态数据集中已存储的选中响应和拒绝响应进行成对比较，而忽略了另一个有用的信号：参考模型自身为相同提示生成的响应。这意味着存在未被利用的有价值信息，可以用于改进策略学习。

Method: 提出Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP)，这是DPO的简单扩展。DPOP在基础偏好损失上增加了一个门控惩罚项，针对参考模型的贪婪响应。该惩罚仅在当前策略对首选响应的似然度仍低于拒绝响应时才激活，从而动态地利用参考模型生成的响应信号。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0基准测试中，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it两个模型上均优于DPO、SimPO和AlphaDPO，长度控制的胜率相对基线分别提升5.3%和4.4%。消融实验进一步表明，SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚在此设置中比NPO和token级不可似然性方法更强。

Conclusion: DPOP成功利用了参考模型生成的响应信号来增强离线偏好优化，通过门控机制有效提升了模型性能，证明了参考模型自身响应作为额外训练信号的价值，为离线偏好学习提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>


### [109] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD)，一种无需验证器的训练方法，通过将基于评分标准条件化的教师模型的token级分布蒸馏到学生模型中，替代了依赖LLM验证器的传统方法，在医疗和科学领域的多个模型上取得了与基于验证器的GRPO方法相当的评分标准满足度，同时消除了训练时的验证器开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于评分标准的训练方法依赖于LLM验证器对每个rollout进行评分，这带来了显著的训练时间开销，使优化过程暴露于验证器特定的偏差，并将评分标准的反馈简化为稀疏的轨迹终点信号。为了解决这些问题，需要一种无需验证器的训练方法。

Method: 提出Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD)方法：将基于评分标准条件化的基础策略模型作为教师模型，无条件化的模型作为学生模型，通过token-by-token的方式将教师分布蒸馏到学生模型中。这种方法用密集的逐token学习信号替代了稀疏的轨迹级奖励，并完全从训练循环中移除了LLM验证器。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B)和Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B)模型上，在医疗和科学领域的测试中，RGSD实现了与基于验证器的GRPO方法相当的评分标准满足度，且每个提示词仅使用一个在线策略rollout，无需训练时验证器调用。消融实验表明原始评分标准提供了比自生成参考响应更强的教师增强信号。

Conclusion: RGSD作为无验证器训练的有效替代方案，在验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈的场景下具有重要价值。虽然更强的GRPO验证器在某些设置下可以超越RGSD，但RGSD作为一个互补的解决方案，能够有效降低训练开销并避免验证器偏差问题。

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [110] [Crossing the Validation Crisis: Cross-Validation Reduces Benchmarking Variance Surprisingly Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12552)
*Célestin Eve,Gaël Varoquaux,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用多重交叉验证分割来提高机器学习基准测试的可靠性，引入了“样本增益”概念量化虚拟数据扩增效果，并提出了动态早停机制以在保证评估置信度的同时提高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习研究严重依赖经验性基准测试，但评估固有的统计变异性（许多算法的随机性加剧了这一问题）常导致性能估计不可靠。有限的测试样本引发“验证危机”，使得真正的算法进步难以辨别。

Method: 1. 提出使用多重交叉验证分割来降低基准测试方差；\n2. 引入“样本增益”（sample gain）概念，量化通过多重交叉验证分割实现的虚拟数据扩增效果；\n3. 提出动态早停程序，通过估计前几个折的样本增益来判断后续折是否带来显著增益，从而决定是否提前终止交叉验证。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集（组织病理学扫描图像和NLP微调任务）上的实验表明，多重交叉验证分割可以显著提高性能估计的可靠性和稳定性，且收益递减的出现往往比预期更晚。动态早停程序能够有效判断后续折的价值。

Conclusion: 研究强调了充分利用交叉验证对可用样本的重要性，以实现稳健可靠的基准测试，从而更好地辨别机器学习算法的真实进展。

Abstract: Modern machine learning progresses through empirical work, benchmarking new methods to evaluate relative performance. However, the statistical variability inherent to evaluation - exacerbated by the stochastic nature of many algorithms - often makes performance estimation unreliable due to the limited test samples available, leading to a validation crisis in which genuine advances are difficult to discern. In this work, we show that cross-validation improves markedly confidence when evaluating and comparing learning algorithm performances. We introduce the concept of sample gain, which quantifies the virtual data augmentation achieved by using multiple cross-validation splits to reduce benchmarking variance. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets (histopathologic scans and NLP fine-tuning) demonstrate that multiple splits can substantially improve the reliability and stability of performance estimates, with diminishing returns often setting in later than expected. We also introduce a procedure to dynamically early-stop cross-validation by estimating from the first few folds if subsequent folds will bring large sample gains. Our findings highlight the value of pushing cross-validation on available samples to achieve robust and reliable benchmarking.

</details>


### [111] [Emerging Flexible Designs for Geospatial Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12595)
*Philipe Dias,Waqwoya Abebe,Abhishek Potnis,Aristeidis Tsaris,Dan Lu,Xiao Wang,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在统一条件下对多种地理空间基础模型架构进行了公平对比，揭示了模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游任务性能之间的设计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型架构多样（包括仅编码器、编码器-解码器、掩码自编码等），缺乏在一致条件下评估其性能权衡的研究，导致难以指导实际模型选择与设计。

Method: 对领先的地理空间多模态基础模型架构进行公平对比；使用相同的自监督学习目标和训练数据集进行预训练；在GEOBench基准上，以一致的参数化设置评估分类和分割任务性能；重点关注模型在不同光谱波段配置下的灵活性。

Result: 在受控条件下揭示了架构的优缺点；提供了关于模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游任务性能之间设计权衡的新见解。

Conclusion: 为构建具备鲁棒多模态推理能力的下一代地理空间基础模型提供了实用的架构选择指导。

Abstract: Foundation models are rapidly transforming Earth observation by enabling scalable pretraining across diverse unlabeled geospatial modalities. However, their architectural diversity ranging from encoder-only to encoder-decoder and masked autoencoding paradigms makes it challenging to assess performance trade offs in a consistent manner. In this work, we present an apples-to-apples comparison of leading FM architectures designed for geospatial multimodal reasoning, with a particular focus on flexibility across varied spectral band configurations. We standardize pretraining using identical self supervised learning objectives and training datasets, and evaluate all models under consistent parameterization on the GEOBench benchmark across classification and segmentation tasks. Our results offer new insights into the design trade-offs between model flexibility, modality alignment, and downstream task performance. By highlighting architectural strengths and limitations under controlled conditions, this study provides practical guidance for building next generation geospatial foundation models capable of robust multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [112] [Viral Proteins Reveal Geometry of Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12609)
*Arthur Bigot,Harmon Bhasin,Core Francisco Park,Eugene Shakhnovich,Dianzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以病毒蛋白为例，研究了蛋白质语言模型在高度不平衡数据集下对代表性不足生物序列的表征，发现模型嵌入空间中存在一个主要的“原生性”轴，该轴根据序列被建模的程度对序列进行排序，同时仍能保留病毒特异性的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型在高度不平衡的数据集上进行训练，但目前尚不清楚它们如何表征那些代表性不足的生物序列。为了回答这一问题，本研究选择病毒蛋白作为典型案例，深入分析模型如何在嵌入空间中处理这类非典型序列。

Method: 研究人员使用ESM系列模型家族，分析了病毒蛋白的嵌入表示。研究重点识别嵌入空间中的主要变化轴，并探讨该轴与掩码重建困惑度（perplexity）的关系。此外，研究还评估了模型规模对不同病毒家族的影响，以及验证病毒蛋白是否在线性可分性上超越零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征。

Result: 研究发现，嵌入空间中存在一个主导的“原生性”轴，该轴与掩码重建困惑度一致，将序列从建模良好的细胞蛋白、病毒蛋白排列到打乱和随机序列。模型规模的扩大不均匀地收缩了这一轴，但病毒蛋白仍然可以被线性分离，超出了仅凭零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征所能解释的范围。

Conclusion: 结果表明，蛋白质语言模型的表征由一个普遍的“原生性”概念所构建，但同时也能够保留不同生物群体的特异性信息，这对理解模型在不平衡数据上的泛化能力和偏见具有重要意义。

Abstract: Protein language models are trained on highly imbalanced datasets, raising the question of how they represent underrepresented biological sequences. Using viral proteins as a case study across ESM model families, we identify a dominant nativeness axis in embedding space, aligned with masked reconstruction perplexity, that orders sequences from well-modeled cellular proteins through viral proteins to shuffled and random sequences. Scaling contracts this axis unevenly across viral families. Despite this, protein language model embeddings retain viral-specific signal: viral proteins remain linearly separable beyond zero-shot perplexity and shallow sequence features. Together, these results suggest that pLM representations are structured by a general notion of nativeness while preserving information specific to distinct biological groups.

</details>


### [113] [The Mathematics of AI Winters: The mathematical Taxonomy of Paradigm Fragility in AI Winter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12610)
*Miquel Noguer i Alonso,David Pacheco Aznar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出人工智能寒冬不仅源于工程失败和期望过高，还源于当时主导范式遇到的数学形式化瓶颈，并通过多个理论结果分析了这些瓶颈及其后续突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的AI寒冬解释主要关注工程失败、商业失望和过度炒作，本文旨在补充一个被忽视的视角：当时的主导范式遭遇了真实的数学形式化障碍。

Method: 通过综合分析多个关键的数学理论结果，包括Minsky和Papert的感知机不可能定理、Blum和Rivest的神经网络训练复杂性理论、Stone的非参数估计极小极大率、Hochreiter等的梯度消失分析，以及Vapnik-Chervonenkis等的经典统计学习理论。

Result: 识别出了早期AI核心失望与数学精确瓶颈之间的对齐关系，包括表示局限性、优化困难、计算复杂性、统计可学习性和高维近似等五大形式化障碍，并阐明了后来的突破如何缓解而非消除这些障碍。

Conclusion: AI寒冬的出现并非单纯的工程或商业失败，而是与当时技术范式所面临的根本性数学限制密切相关；后续的突破通过缓解这些瓶颈推动了AI发展，但并未完全消除这些根本性障碍。

Abstract: Two major periods of reduced funding and confidence in artificial intelligence research, commonly called the first and second AI winters, are usually explained through engineering failure, commercial disappointment, and inflated expectations. This article develops a complementary thesis: that the dominant paradigms of those periods also met genuine formal barriers, including limitations of representation, optimisation, computational complexity, statistical learnability, and high-dimensional approximation. The contribution is synthetic rather than archival. We do not claim that particular theorems mechanically caused the winters; rather, we show that several central disappointments of early AI were aligned with mathematically precise bottlenecks. We analyse these bottlenecks through the perceptron impossibility results of Minsky and Papert, the complexity-theoretic hardness of exact neural-network training established by Blum and Rivest, minimax rates for nonparametric estimation in high dimension due to Stone, vanishing-gradient analyses by Hochreiter and by Bengio and collaborators, and classical statistical learning theory in the tradition of Vapnik and Chervonenkis, Valiant, and Blumer and collaborators. We then relate these barriers to the later breakthroughs that mitigated, rather than eliminated, them.

</details>


### [114] [Evaluation of AutoML Frameworks for IDS under Imbalanced Data Conditions of the NSL-KDD Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12611)
*Wiliane Carolina Silva,Evandro César Vilas Boas,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了九个开源AutoML框架在严重类别不平衡的NSL-KDD五类网络入侵检测任务中的表现，发现集成学习和不平衡感知优化能显著提升少数类检测性能，PyCaret表现最佳（macro-F1达66%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多通过二分类或移除少数类来简化问题，缺乏对AutoML框架在真实多类严重不平衡场景下性能的系统评估。本研究旨在填补这一空白，评估AutoML在保留原始五类分布（包含R2L和U2R等高度欠代表攻击）时的不平衡敏感学习行为。

Method: 在统一可复现的实验协议下，分析九个开源AutoML框架。考虑框架在架构设计、集成策略、验证流程、超参数优化和不平衡处理机制方面的差异，保持NSL-KDD数据集原始五类分布，重点关注少数类攻击的检测能力。

Result: 具备集成学习和不平衡感知优化的框架表现更优。PyCaret以66%的macro-F1取得最佳整体性能，AutoGluon以55%位居第二。缺乏原生平衡支持的框架在少数类检测上显著退化。单纯追求准确率优化的框架在高度不平衡场景下泛化能力不足。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了AutoML入侵检测在严重多类不平衡下的标准化基准，揭示了当前架构局限性。结果表明需要将不平衡感知优化、重采样和分层评估策略原生集成到自动化学习流程中，而非仅依赖准确率导向的优化。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of severe class imbalance on the performance of automated machine learning (AutoML) frameworks for multiclass network intrusion detection using the NSL-KDD dataset. Unlike previous studies that simplify the problem through binary classification or minority-class removal, we preserve the original five-class distribution, including highly underrepresented attacks such as R2L and U2R, enabling a realistic evaluation of imbalance-sensitive learning behavior. Nine open-source AutoML frameworks were analyzed under a unified and reproducible experimental protocol, considering differences in architectural design, ensemble strategies, validation procedures, hyperparameter optimization, and imbalance-handling mechanisms. The results demonstrate that frameworks incorporating ensemble learning and imbalance-aware optimization achieve better minority-class discrimination. PyCaret obtained the best overall performance, reaching 66\% macro-F1, followed by AutoGluon with 55\%, whereas frameworks lacking native balancing support exhibited significant degradation in minority-class detection capability. The analysis further shows that accuracy-oriented optimization alone is insufficient for highly imbalanced IDS scenarios, since high-weighted metrics may coexist with poor generalization on rare attack categories. As a contribution, this work establishes a standardized benchmark for AutoML-based intrusion detection under severe multiclass imbalance, highlighting current architectural limitations and the need for native integration of imbalance-aware optimization, resampling, and stratified evaluation strategies into automated learning pipelines. The source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [115] [Towards Provably Fair Machine Learning: Bayesian Approaches For Consistent and Transparent Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12615)
*Owen O'Neill,Fintan Costello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出公平贝叶斯分类器，通过强制确定性和统计一致性要求解决机器学习模型在细粒度子群体上预测不一致的问题，在三个基准数据集上实现零一致性误差同时保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域中机器学习分类器对不同子群体的预测质量存在系统性差异，尤其是由多特征交集定义的细粒度子群体。正则化会通过将小子群体合并到更大群体来改善整体性能，但对人口统计学少数群体造成不成比例的影响。少数群体集中在小子群体中，而这些群体中频率推断最不可靠，这是算法公平性的关键问题。

Method: 定义两个一致性预测要求：(1)确定性——相同个体获得相同预测；(2)统计一致性——在显著性水平α下，无法拒绝子群体预测来自该子群体贝叶斯最优目标分布的假设。基于这些要求推导出公平贝叶斯分类器，同时在每个群体和子群体上强制执行这两个要求，并在无法做出一致性确定性预测时弃权。

Result: 在Adult、COMPAS和Bank Marketing三个基准数据集上，标准分类器对大量子群体产生统计不一致的预测。所提出的分类器通过构造实现零一致性误差，同时在每个测试数据集上超越基线准确率和多校准性能。

Conclusion: 统计一致性为预测质量提供了原则性基础，对算法公平性具有直接影响。通过在数据支持的最细粒度上强制执行贝叶斯一致性，证明了带有原则性弃权的穷尽性子群体公平性在实践中是可实现的。解决小子群体推断问题是实现公平机器学习的必要步骤。

Abstract: ML classifiers deployed in high-stakes domains produce predictions whose quality varies systematically across subgroups. For granular subgroups defined by intersections of multiple features, predictions are often inconsistent with the observed data: the model's outputs contradict the evidence available for that subgroup. This problem is exacerbated by regularisation, which improves aggregate performance by collapsing small subgroups into larger groups, disproportionately affecting demographic minorities. We define two requirements for consistent prediction: determinism (identical individuals receive identical predictions) and statistical consistency (we cannot reject, at significance level alpha, the hypothesis that the predictions for a subgroup were drawn from the Bayesian optimal target distribution inferred for that subgroup). From these requirements we derive the Fair Bayesian classifier, which enforces both across every group and subgroup simultaneously and abstains whenever no consistent deterministic prediction is possible. On three benchmark datasets (Adult, COMPAS, and Bank Marketing), standard classifiers produce statistically inconsistent predictions for a substantial proportion of subgroups. Our classifier achieves zero consistency error by construction while exceeding baseline accuracy and multicalibration on every dataset tested. Statistical consistency provides a principled foundation for prediction quality with direct implications for algorithmic fairness. Minority demographics are disproportionately concentrated in small subgroups, precisely where frequentist inference is least reliable; addressing this inference problem is therefore a necessary step toward fair ML. By enforcing Bayesian consistency at the finest resolution the data supports, the our classifier demonstrates that exhaustive subgroup fairness with principled abstention is achievable in practice.

</details>


### [116] [Bag of Dims: Training-Free Mechanistic Interpretability via Dimension-Level Sign Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12629)
*Varun Reddy Nalagatla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现Transformer隐藏状态的标准基（单个维度）本身就是一个免训练、架构通用的特征基。单个维度通过符号编码语义内容，通过幅度编码置信度，可独立作为二进制寄存器。无需训练即可实现高效的特征读取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer隐藏状态的标准基是否本身就能作为有效的特征表示，而无需依赖训练得到的基向量或分解方法，从而简化特征发现流程。

Method: 提出Bag of Dims框架，在三个模型家族（Qwen 3.5-4B、Gemma 3-4B、Mistral 7B）上进行四组递进实验：1）仅保留符号信息测试预测能力；2）使用单次前向传播的类型缓存发现语义特征；3）训练探测头并与轴对齐权重对比；4）在K、V投影和FFN权重中验证特征可发现性。

Result: 符号模式单独即可承载预测内容：将所有幅度置1后，LM head达到72-93% top-5下一词元准确率，纯汉明评分达80-90% top-4096。发现175个语义类别（平均AUC 0.80），训练探测头仅提升+0.018 AUC并收敛至轴对齐权重。20%的特征与单个写入神经元相关（>0.70一致性），top-200神经元联盟对99.9%原型达成>0.70一致性。完全无监督发现可扩展至1500特征（100%产出率、99%稀疏度）。

Conclusion: 标准基足以支撑整个Transformer计算路径的特征读取，无需训练、优化或大量计算资源，仅需每个词汇词元一次前向传播，验证了隐藏状态维度的独立性和解耦性。

Abstract: We show that the standard basis of transformer hidden states already provides a training-free, architecture-general feature basis. Individual dimensions encode semantic content via their signs and confidence via their magnitudes, functioning as independent binary registers. We validate this Bag of Dims framework across three model families (Qwen 3.5-4B, Gemma 3-4B, Mistral 7B) through four progressive experiments. Sign patterns alone carry predictive content: replacing all magnitudes with unity achieves 72-93% top-5 next-token accuracy through the LM head, and pure Hamming scoring without any decoder reaches 80-90% top-4096. These sign patterns organize into semantic features: using a single-token type cache (one forward pass per vocabulary token, no context), we discover 175 categories via per-dimension sign consistency (mean AUC 0.80) from 50 anchors with zero training. A trained probe adds only +0.018 AUC and converges to axis-aligned weights, confirming negligible cross-dimension structure. This structure extends to attention: all 175 categories remain discoverable in K and V projections. On the write side, static FFN weight inspection links 20% of features to individual writer neurons (>0.70 agreement; random controls: 0%), with top-200 neuron coalitions achieving >0.70 agreement on 99.9% of prototypes via majority vote. Fully unsupervised discovery (random seeds, no labels) scales to 1500 features at 100% yield and 99% sparsity across all three models, with pairwise MI of 0.0014 bits confirming low inter-dimension coupling. These results establish that the standard basis already suffices for feature reading throughout the transformer compute pathway, requiring no training, no optimization, and no GPU-days beyond a single forward pass per vocabulary token.

</details>


### [117] [Keep Policy Gradient in Charge: Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12634)
*Tianyu Ding,Jianhong Xin,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SGCD方法，通过兄弟轨迹对比生成细粒度信用分配，解决了长视界工具使用RL中信号稀疏和自蒸馏盲目模仿的问题，在AppWorld和τ³-airline基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界工具使用强化学习存在轨迹级优势信号稀疏的问题，而直接的token级自蒸馏会盲目模仿教师行为，无法区分有用技能和有害捷径，反而可能破坏工具使用能力。

Method: 提出Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation (SGCD)：1)动态采样生成成功/失败的兄弟轨迹；2)外部LLM总结对比生成逐步信用参考；3)基于教师-学生差异进行信用重分配；4)有界的分离信用权重重塑GRPO token优势。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上，test_normal从42.9提升至45.6，test_challenge从24.7提升至27.0；在τ³-airline基准上，pass@1从0.583提升至0.602。

Conclusion: SGCD通过将蒸馏重新定位为信用分配工具而非竞争性演员损失，有效解决了长视界工具使用中的信号稀疏问题，避免了直接自蒸馏的盲目模仿陷阱，为密集信号学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-use reinforcement learning can learn from outcome verification, but its
  trajectory-level advantage is broadcast across many reasoning, API, and answer tokens.
  Self-distillation promises a denser signal by reusing a policy's own rollouts or a privileged
  teacher. We show, however, that direct token-level self-distillation can silently destroy tool use:
  it rehearses teacher behavior without knowing which actions the verifier rewards, so useful skills
  and harmful shortcuts are amplified together. We introduce Sibling-Guided Credit Distillation
  (SGCD), which uses distillation for credit assignment rather than as a competing actor loss.
  Dynamic sampling produces mixed successful and failed sibling rollouts; an external LLM summarizes
  their contrast into a training-only stepwise credit reference; dense teacher/student divergence
  drives credit reassignment; and bounded detached credit weights reshape GRPO token advantages. The
  deployed student sees no external LLM, sibling evidence, or oracle. Across AppWorld and
  $τ^3$-airline, SGCD improves over matched GRPO comparators: AppWorld TGC $42.9 \to 45.6$ on
  test_normal and $24.7 \to 27.0$ on test_challenge, and $τ^3$-airline pass@1 $0.583 \to 0.602$.

</details>


### [118] [The Metric Picks the Winner: Evaluation Choice Flips Model Rankings for Drug-Response Prediction in Unseen Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12639)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Riya Bisht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在真实药物化学的留出测试中，指标选择决定模型排名：深度学习模型在活性集指标下显著优于简单基线，而在代理指标下则相反，首次在药物领域验证了指标校准效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测细胞对新药物的转录组反应是计算细胞生物学的核心难题，近期基准测试表明复杂模型在化学留出测试中往往无法超越简单基线，需要深入研究模型性能评估的度量标准问题。

Method: 提出分阶段方法：简单基线（未处理对照和训练化合物平均响应）、非参数检索（Tanimoto加权平均最近邻化合物）、融合阶段（结合化学嵌入与检索特征预测残差，包含不确定性头部和基因程序）。使用Morgan指纹的正则化线性回归作为对比基线。

Result: 在Bemis-Murcko骨架分割下，使用代理指标时线性回归胜过深度模型；但使用竞赛真实活性集指标时，深度模型获胜，融合解码器显著优于线性基线（-0.012 wMSE，p<10^-4），代理指标的优胜者变为最差的化学感知预测器。

Conclusion: 指标选择决定模型排名，这是首次在真实留出药物化学上验证了指标校准效应（之前主要在基因扰动研究中建立），强调了评估度量对药物反应预测模型选择的关键影响。

Abstract: Predicting how a cell's transcriptome responds to a drug it has never seen is a core, hard problem in computational cell biology: recent benchmarks show complex models often fail to beat trivial baselines once test compounds are held out by chemistry. We study one cell line and assay, THP-1 cells profiled by DRUG-seq, scored by the active-compound weighted MSE(wMSE) of the VCPI prediction contest. We propose a staged approach: dumb baselines (untreated control and mean training-compound response) that the field keeps failing to beat; non-parametric retrieval (a Tanimoto-weighted average of a held-out compound's nearest training compounds); and a fusion stage combining a frozen chemistry embedding with retrieval-support features to predict the residual over the mean, with an uncertainty head and gene programs. On the released VCPI THP-1 drug-seq data (14,026 training compounds), under a Bemis-Murcko scaffold split, the model ranking inverts depending on the metric. Under an inverse-variance per-gene proxy, a regularized linear regression on Morgan fingerprints appears to win over the deep models, retrieval, and ChemBERTa -- the textbook "simple baselines win" result. But under the contest's true active-set metric (per-(gene, compound) Mejia weights, validated against the official scorer; mean baseline 0.535 vs the organizers' 0.507 reference), that reverses: the deep models win, our fusion decoder significantly beats the linear fingerprint baseline (-0.012 wMSE, paired bootstrap p < 10^-4), and the proxy's winner becomes the worst chemistry-aware predictor. Picking the metric picks the winner -- to our knowledge the first demonstration on real held-out drug chemistry of the metric-calibration effect established largely on genetic perturbation. We release a reproducible pipeline
  wired to the official scorer that emits a valid submission over the real 1064 x 12,995 grid.

</details>


### [119] [Individual Control Barrier Functions-Guided Diffusion Model for Safe Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12640)
*Qingyun Guo,Junyi Shi,Jianuo Huang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种安全的离线多智能体强化学习算法，通过将神经个体控制障碍函数嵌入扩散模型来增强轨迹生成过程中的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线强化学习方法主要关注单智能体场景，而多智能体环境中的安全性挑战尚未得到充分探索。在安全关键任务中，如何保证多智能体系统的安全性是一个重要问题。

Method: 将神经个体控制障碍函数嵌入到扩散模型中，在轨迹生成过程中增强安全性，并通过逆动力学恢复控制策略。

Result: 在多个不同的基准测试中验证了算法的有效性，结果显示在保持竞争性奖励的同时，安全性得到了显著改善。

Conclusion: 所提出的安全离线多智能体强化学习算法能够有效提升多智能体系统的安全性，同时保持良好的性能表现，为安全关键任务提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning allows control policies to be learned directly from data without online interaction, making it suitable for safety-critical tasks. Recent studies have applied diffusion models to offline reinforcement learning to leverage their strong capacity for modeling complex data distributions. However, existing approaches primarily focus on single-agent settings, leaving the safety challenges in multi-agent environments largely unexplored. In this work, we propose a safe offline multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm that embeds neural individual control barrier functions into the diffusion model to enhance safety during trajectory generation, with control policies recovered through inverse dynamics. We evaluate our algorithm across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating substantial safety improvements while maintaining competitive rewards.

</details>


### [120] [TEDD: Robust Detection of Unstable Temporal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12643)
*Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Bruno Casal Laraña,Nádia Soares,Miguel Araújo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TEDD技术，通过回归模型预测实例时间戳，自动检测导致机器学习模型性能下降的时变特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界时序数据中特征分布会随时间变化，直接应用机器学习模型会导致性能快速退化，因此需要自动识别随时间变化的特征以构建更鲁棒的模型。

Method: 提出TEDD技术，利用回归模型预测实例的时间戳，通过分析特征对时间戳预测的贡献度来识别不稳定特征；定义了特征不应遭受的时序变化类型，可检测数值和类别型特征的单变量及多变量漂移。

Result: 在真实和合成数据上的实验表明，该方法能够检测所有基本变化类型（数值和类别特征）、检测多变量漂移、返回可比较的特征变化量度量、无需参数调整，并在特征数和数据实例数上具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: TEDD方法能够有效识别导致机器学习模型不稳定的时变特征，帮助数据科学家采取数据变换等措施，部署能够长期保持高性能的更鲁棒模型。

Abstract: When working with real-world temporal data, it is common to encounter features whose distribution is changing over time. The naive employment of Machine Learning models on this unstable data might lead to rapidly degrading performance, especially if the new distribution is much different from what was previously seen during training. In order to cope with this problem, it is critical to automatically identify features that are changing over time. With these features detected, data scientists and other practitioners will be able to mitigate the issue (for instance, by applying data transformations), deploying more robust models that retain high performance for longer periods of time. In this paper, we describe which temporal changes a feature should not suffer from, and propose TEDD, a technique to a) identify when a dataset might lead to an unstable Machine Learning model and b) automatically detect which features cause such lack of robustness. In order to achieve it, we leverage a regression model to highlight which features contribute to a good prediction of an instance's timestamp. We compare our approach to other methods in real and synthetic data, testing their detection capability on all simple change patterns. We show that our method: detects all types of basic changes, both for numerical and categorical features; can detect multivariate drifts; returns a comparable value measuring the amount of change of each feature; requires no parameter tuning; and is scalable both on number of features and instances of the dataset.

</details>


### [121] [Physics-Aware Auxiliary Losses Improve Out-of-Distribution Generalization of a GNN Synthesizability Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12651)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在图神经网络上引入物理先验（拓扑复杂度和应变能）作为辅助监督信号，以提升分子可合成性预测在分布外场景下的泛化能力。实验表明，相较于基线模型，物理感知变体在OOD测试集上有统计显著的小幅提升（最佳组合提升Δ=+0.0066），但在分布内数据上无显著差异。研究同时强调了多种子实验对验证结果可靠性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习药物发现管道越来越依赖生成模型，这些模型提出的分子往往远离下游可合成性过滤器的训练数据。现有的可合成性过滤器（SAScore、SCScore、RAscore、DeepSA）纯粹基于统计方法，在分布外（OOD）场景下性能会显著下降。研究者希望探索廉价、闭式物理先验作为图神经网络的辅助监督是否能改善OOD泛化能力。

Method: 在GINE主干网络上添加两个辅助损失函数：（1）由Bertz指数监督的拓扑复杂度回归；（2）由MMFF94力场能量监督的应变能软惩罚。使用65,177个分子语料库（HIV、Tox21、COCONUT），按SAScore阈值标注。在单源OOD划分上进行4路消融实验（基线/+复杂度/+应变/+两者），在HIV+Tox21上训练，在COCONUT天然产物上测试，使用5个种子重复实验并配对自举置信区间。

Result: 三种物理感知变体在OOD测试上均获得统计显著的小幅提升（基线OOD AUC=0.9774）：+复杂度（Δ=+0.0060，95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102]）、+应变（Δ=+0.0032，CI [+0.0008, +0.0052]）、+两者（Δ=+0.0066，CI [+0.0038, +0.0093]）。每个置信区间均不包含零，组合效果最佳。在分布内数据上各变体表现无显著差异，仅在OOD评估下可见效果。研究同时发现：单种子版本实验产生了定性不同的非单调结果，未能通过多种子验证。

Conclusion: 廉价物理先验作为辅助监督可以显著改善分子可合成性预测的OOD泛化能力，虽然效果幅度较小但统计显著。研究明确指出效应温和，并警示了单种子实验可能产生误导性结论的方法论问题，强调了多种子实验对结果可靠性的重要性。该工作为机器学习药物发现中统计方法的OOD局限性提供了物理先验改进的有效路径。

Abstract: Machine-learning drug-discovery pipelines increasingly rely on generative models that propose molecules far from the data used to train downstream synthesizability filters. Existing filters (SAScore, SCScore, RAscore, DeepSA) are purely statistical and degrade in exactly this out-of-distribution (OOD) regime. We ask whether cheap, closed-form physical priors, used as auxiliary supervision on a graph neural network (GNN), improve OOD generalization. We add two auxiliary losses to a GINE backbone: a topological complexity regression supervised by the Bertz index, and a strain-energy soft penalty supervised by MMFF94 force-field energy. On a 65,177-molecule corpus (HIV, Tox21, COCONUT) labeled by SAScore thresholds we reproduce a strong in-distribution baseline, then evaluate a 4-way ablation (baseline / +complexity / +strain / +both) on a single-source OOD split (train on drug-like HIV+Tox21, test on COCONUT natural products), repeated over 5 seeds with paired bootstrap confidence intervals. All three physics-aware variants give a small but statistically significant OOD improvement over the baseline (mean OOD AUC 0.9774): +complexity Delta = +0.0060 (95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102]), +strain Delta = +0.0032 ([+0.0008, +0.0052]), +both Delta = +0.0066 ([+0.0038, +0.0093]); every interval excludes zero, and the combination is best. The variants are indistinguishable in-distribution, so the effect is visible only under OOD evaluation. We are explicit that the effects are modest, and we report a cautionary methodological finding: a single-seed version of this experiment produced a qualitatively different (non-monotone) story that did not survive multi-seed evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在化疗药物动力学问题中的表现，发现PINNs能够匹配传统方法在线性问题上的性能，同时暴露模型的结构不可识别性问题，并能整合异质测量数据，提供了一种统一的建模方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物学的部分观测问题中，某些隔室无法测量，但控制动力学已知。化疗药物动力学就是一个典型例子：血浆药物浓度常规测量，但决定肿瘤杀伤和脱靶毒性的组织浓度无法测量。需要一种方法能够同时处理血浆数据和组织数据的推断问题。

Method: 将PINN与两个基准进行比较：(1)标准临床基线——对解析双指数血浆解的非线性最小二乘法(NLS)；(2)物理无关的神经基线——仅数据的MLP。在两个药代动力学问题上进行测试：线性两隔室问题和Michaelis-Menten扩展(饱和消除)问题。

Result: 在线性两隔室问题上，NLS接近最优，PINN以小常数因子匹配其性能，同时单次训练即可生成组织曲线，而数据MLP在组织上失败约10倍。在Michaelis-Menten扩展问题上，双指数闭式解不存在，NLS误设并返回无意义常数。PINN暴露了从血浆单独测量该模型不可识别的事实。添加两个稀疏组织观测后，PINN在五个种子上恢复k21至真值1%以内，Vmax和Km在一个标准差内，而NLS因双指数假设仅描述血浆而无法尝试恢复。

Conclusion: PINNs的价值不在于击败NLS，而在于提供统一方法：在教科书问题上匹配教科书估计器，暴露教科书估计器隐藏的结构可识别性问题，并在单个损失函数中吸收异质测量。PINNs为部分观测的生物动力学问题提供了一种诚实且通用的建模工具。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [123] [A Zero-shot Generalized Graph Anomaly Detection Framework via Node Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12673)
*Phan Nguyen,Dat Cao,Hien Chu,Khue Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlignGAD框架，通过全局统一、聚类和节点差异评分三个模块，实现了跨领域的零样本图异常检测，无需目标图数据即可识别异常节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨领域图异常检测方法通常依赖于特定数据集的特征语义和结构模式，这严重限制了模型在不同领域之间的泛化能力。因此，研究如何在异构图数据中实现具有强泛化能力的零样本图异常检测成为一个重要挑战。

Method: 本文提出了AlignGAD框架，包含三个核心模块：（1）全局统一模块，用于对齐异构节点特征并在谱域归一化图信号；（2）聚类模块，构建聚类感知的图视图以捕获群体层面的异常模式；（3）节点差异评分模块，测量重构差异并聚合来自不同图视图的异常证据。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，AlignGAD在零样本图异常检测设置下具有显著的有效性，能够成功识别未见目标图中的异常节点。

Conclusion: AlignGAD框架成功解决了跨领域图异常检测中的泛化难题，通过特征对齐、聚类视图构建和差异评分的创新设计，实现了无需目标图训练数据的零样本异常检测，为处理异构图数据的实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to identify abnormal nodes in unseen target graphs, showing strong potential in real-world applications with heterogeneous graph data. However, existing methods often depend on dataset-specific feature semantics and structural patterns, which limits their ability to generalize across different domains. To address this challenge, we propose AlignGAD, a zero-shot generalized graph anomaly detection framework. Our framework is built upon three key components: a Global Unification Module that aligns heterogeneous node features and normalizes graph signals in the spectral domain; a Clustering Module that constructs cluster-aware graph views to capture group-level abnormal patterns; and a Node Discrepancy Scoring Module that measures reconstruction discrepancy and aggregates anomaly evidence from different graph views. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AlignGAD under the zero-shot GAD setting.

</details>


### [124] [Fed-FBD: Federated Functional Block Diversification for Isolation, Privacy, and Surgical Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12679)
*Weijie Chen,Alan B. McMillan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fed-FBD，一种模块化联邦学习架构，通过将ResNet分解为功能块并提供多种“颜色变体”，实现了对抗客户端隔离、隐私保护设计和快速机器遗忘功能，仅需付出轻微精度代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习方法（如FedAvg）将客户端视为黑盒，缺乏隔离恶意贡献者、审计客户端影响力或尊重退出参与者“被遗忘权”的机制，存在安全隐患和隐私风险。

Method: 将ResNet骨干网络分解为六个功能块（茎部、四个残差组和分类头），维护N个颜色变体仓库，每个变体由独立追踪和标记贡献者的模块组装而成。这种设计确保了块级隔离、隐私保护和精确遗忘。

Result: 在MedMNIST-2D、PathMNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验显示，Fed-FBD在充分数据集上仅付出0.3%-3.1%的IID精度差距，在Dirichlet alpha=1.0设置下与FedAvg相差0.8%-4.0%，并能将所有六种对抗攻击限制在受感染客户端自身模块内，干净颜色的AUC漂移最多仅为±0.01。

Conclusion: Fed-FBD成功地在联邦学习中引入了架构级别的隔离保障、隐私保护和机器遗忘能力，证明了通过模块化设计和适当的精度权衡，可以显著提升联邦学习系统的安全性和可控性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw patient data, but standard approaches such as FedAvg treat each client as a black box and provide no mechanism for isolating an adversarial contributor, auditing per-client influence, or honoring a departed participant's right to be forgotten. We present Fed-FBD (Federated Functional Block Diversification), a modular federated architecture that decomposes a ResNet backbone into six functional blocks (the stem, four residual groups, and the classification head) and maintains a warehouse of N color variants, each assembled from independently tracked and contributor-stamped blocks. Fed-FBD provides three capabilities absent in FedAvg: (i) architecturally guaranteed block-level isolation, so that an adversarial or mislabelled client cannot contaminate the clean colous; (ii) privacy-by-design, where membership inference advantage is already indistinguishable from chance before any privacy mechanism is applied; and (iii) surgical machine unlearning of a departed participant's contribution at sub-second cost and without retraining. Experiments on six MedMNIST-2D datasets, PathMNIST at 224x224, and CIFAR-10 show that Fed-FBD trades a modest 0.3%-3.1% IID accuracy gap on the adequately sized datasets for these guarantees, remains within 0.8%-4.0% of FedAvg at Dirichlet alpha=1.0 on three of four datasets, and confines all six adversarial attacks we study to the poisoned client's own blocks with at most +/-0.01 AUC drift on the clean colors.

</details>


### [125] [How Useful is Causal Invariance for Domain Adaptation in Finite-Sample Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12680)
*Julia Kostin,Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboim,Samory Kpotufe,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了因果知识在有监督域自适应(sDA)中的有限样本增益，发现目标风险边际和源估计误差决定了因果知识是否能带来改进，并在边际足够大时提出了自适应聚合算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果域泛化研究主要关注群体层面的因果不变性，但在有限样本设置下(特别是有少量目标域标注样本的有监督域自适应场景)，因果不变性预测器能否带来实际增益仍未被充分探索。

Method: 在线性回归框架下，利用完整或部分因果知识确定不变特征子集，得到源域训练的候选预测器集合；推导目标风险边际与源估计误差的上下界，提出自适应聚合算法。

Result: 证明了当目标风险边际相对于n_Q足够大时，自适应聚合算法能达到最优候选预测器性能且避免负迁移；边际过小时任何算法都无法可靠利用候选预测器获得更快收敛率；建立了边际与线性SCM中结构偏移幅度的联系。

Conclusion: 因果知识对有监督域自适应的有限样本增益取决于目标风险边际：只有边际足够大时才能获得理论保证的改进，为实践中应用因果知识提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Machine learning models often degrade when they are deployed on a target distribution that differs from the source distributions they were trained on. Recent work in causality-based domain generalization has shown how shared causal structure between domains can induce invariant predictors, e.g., models on a subset of features which have stable risk across structured domain shifts. However, the extent to which such population-level causal invariances can lead to gains in finite-sample settings remains underexplored. In particular, in practice we often have access to a few labeled target samples, a setting called supervised domain adaptation (sDA). In this paper, we explore when (full or partial) causal knowledge can provably improve supervised domain adaptation.
  As a first step, we study linear regression, where full or partial causal knowledge specifies a collection of invariant or possibly invariant feature subsets, each yielding a source-trained candidate predictor. We derive matching upper and lower bounds showing that finite-sample gains are governed by the target-risk margins separating the candidates, together with the finite-source estimation error. When these margins are sufficiently large relative to $n_Q$, an adaptive aggregation procedure can match the best candidate predictor while avoiding negative transfer relative to target-only learning. On the other hand, when the margins are too small, no algorithm can reliably exploit the candidate collection to obtain faster finite-sample rates. We further connect these margins to structural shift magnitude in linear SCMs and validate the theory on real-world causal benchmarks.

</details>


### [126] [Forecasting Is Not Attribution: Localizing Decoder Bypass in Graph-Based Neural Marketing Mix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12687)
*Yunbo Wang,Bolbi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了图神经网络营销组合模型中存在的"归因旁路"问题——模型可以获得很低的预测误差但无法正确进行归因分析。提出了DICE-MMM框架，通过两阶段训练和诊断测试证明了预测准确性与归因能力不等价，发现关键瓶颈在于图结构选择而非预测能力或解码器容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销组合模型同时用于预测业务结果和将结果归因于营销渠道，但这两个目标并不等价。研究者发现在基于图的神经网络MMM中存在归因旁路的失败模式：高容量解码器可以通过目标自回归、密集通信、协同运动、上下文或潜在记忆获得低预测误差，但无法通过用于归因的图路由反事实敏感性。

Method: 提出DICE-MMM作为有界诊断和训练框架，将三个常被混淆的问题分离：图恢复、预测准确性、训练解码器的扰动诱导影响是否与图对齐。第一阶段用受限的图介导解码器训练图编码器；第二阶段冻结选定编码器，训练图安全潜在解码器（其跨节点通信必须通过提供的图）。使用CIG、AR-CIG和图交换测试评估解码器。

Result: 在受控的R/d/T交换和外部多图原始日志压力测试中，DICE相比CausalMMM改进了稳定的图恢复。实验证明预测准确性不是归因证书：在稀疏目标基准测试中，无图和全图解码器在MSE@7约0.004时AR-CIG nAUPRC接近或低于零，而oracle图在相近MSE下达到0.807±0.129。冻结图交换定位了瓶颈：同样的DICE硬训练解码器从学习图输入下的nAUPRC -0.044±0.006变为oracle图下的0.894±0.027。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个压力测试和失败定位框架，表明低MSE可以隐藏归因旁路问题，且未解决的瓶颈是图支撑选择，而非预测或解码器容量。这为营销组合模型的实际应用提供了重要的诊断工具和方法论指导。

Abstract: Marketing mix models are used to forecast business outcomes and to attribute those outcomes to marketing channels, but these goals are not equivalent. We study a failure mode in graph-based neural MMM called attribution bypass: a high-capacity decoder can obtain low forecasting error through target autoregression, dense communication, co-movement, context, or latent memory while failing to route counterfactual sensitivity through the graph used as the attribution object. We introduce DICE-MMM as a bounded diagnostic and training framework. We do not claim that observational neural MMM identifies causal effects. Instead, DICE separates three questions often conflated in graph-based MMM: graph recovery, forecasting accuracy, and whether the trained decoder's perturbation-induced influence is graph aligned. Stage 1 trains a graph encoder with a restricted graph-mediated decoder. Stage 2 freezes the selected encoder and trains a graph-safe latent decoder whose cross-node communication must pass through the supplied graph. Decoder use is evaluated with CIG, AR-CIG, and graph-swap tests. Across controlled R/d/T swaps and an external multi-graph rawlog stress test, DICE improves stable graph recovery over CausalMMM. The experiments show that forecasting accuracy is not an attribution certificate: in a sparse-target benchmark, no-graph and full-graph decoders achieve MSE@7 around 0.004 while AR-CIG nAUPRC remains near or below zero, whereas an oracle graph reaches 0.807 +/- 0.129 at comparable MSE. Frozen graph-swap localizes the bottleneck: the same DICE-hard-trained decoder moves from nAUPRC -0.044 +/- 0.006 under learned graph inputs to 0.894 +/- 0.027 with the oracle graph. The contribution is a stress test and failure-localization framework showing that low MSE can hide attribution bypass and that the unresolved bottleneck is graph-support selection, not forecasting or decoder capacity.

</details>


### [127] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在部分观测线性动力系统数据上训练的两层线性自回归模型，能够自然地逼近卡尔曼滤波，其学习到的隐层表示与最优卡尔曼滤波器产生的状态估计在相似变换下一致，并提供了有限样本理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在序列数据建模中表现优异，但理论上理解其如何以及为何学习到潜在表示仍是一个开放问题。本文旨在揭示自回归模型学习潜在表示的内在机制。

Method: 研究在部分观测线性动力系统数据上，通过经验风险最小化训练的两层线性自回归模型。主要包含三个理论洞察：1）证明卡尔曼滤波器可被自回归模型良好逼近且有界截断误差；2）分析优化景观的非凸性，证明所有驻点要么是严格鞍点要么是全局最小值；3）提供有限样本理论保证。

Result: 证明了学习到的隐层表示在相似变换下与最优卡尔曼滤波器产生的状态估计完全一致；提供了预测误差、参数估计误差和潜在状态恢复的有限样本保证；数值模拟验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 尽管模型没有底层动力学或状态的显式知识，自回归模型仍能自然地学习到与卡尔曼滤波等价的状态估计，这为理解自回归模型的表示学习机制提供了理论洞察。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [128] [LLM-Powered Personalized Glycemic Assessment in Type 2 Diabetes with Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12699)
*Yifan Gao,Yanmin Gong,Yun Shi,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GlyLLM框架，利用大语言模型整合可穿戴传感器数据（CGM、健身追踪器）和结构化元数据，在血糖预测任务上RMSE平均提升13.66%，在糖尿病分类任务上AUROC平均提升13.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2型糖尿病（T2D）对全球健康构成日益严重的威胁，需要有效的血糖评估支持个性化糖尿病护理。现有方法主要基于传统机器学习，仅依赖历史血糖测量而忽视个性化信息，限制了其在不同糖尿病人群中的性能。可穿戴传感器虽然提供了宝贵见解，但需要与个体层面的关键信息整合才能有效分析。

Method: 提出GlyLLM框架，这是一个基于大语言模型的框架，通过整合可穿戴传感器数据和结构化元数据来建模基于CGM的血糖动态。该框架利用预训练LLM的广泛先验知识，在决策时实现传感器-文本语义抽象。在AI-READI数据集上进行实验，并进行了消融研究以分析不同健康信息的重要性。

Result: 在AI-READI数据集的两个相关任务上：（1）血糖预测任务中RMSE平均提升13.66%；（2）糖尿病分类任务中AUROC平均提升13.08%。消融研究表明，糖尿病问卷调查和生物特征测试比其他健康信息对血糖评估更为关键。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了利用大语言模型推进T2D护理中个性化血糖评估的可行性，为整合多模态数据和个性化信息提供了新的解决方案，是朝着个性化糖尿病护理迈出的重要一步。

Abstract: Type 2 Diabetes (T2D) poses an increasing global health threat, demanding effective glycemic assessment to support personalized and improved diabetes care. Wearable sensors such as continuous glucose monitors (CGM) and fitness trackers offer many valuable insights for glycemic assessment. However, effectively analyzing these data requires integration with essential individual-level context. Existing methods are often based on traditional machine learning (ML) and rely primarily on historical blood glucose measurements and overlook personalized information, which limits their performance across diverse diabetes populations. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated their ability to integrate diverse data modalities while modeling sequential dependencies, motivating the exploration of their potential for personalized glycemic assessment.
  In this paper, we propose GlyLLM, an LLM-powered framework for modeling CGM-based glycemic dynamics through the integration of wearable sensor data and structured metadata. GlyLLM can leverage the extensive prior knowledge of pre-trained LLMs and achieve sensor-text semantic abstraction at decision time. Experiments on two related tasks on the AI-READI dataset demonstrate that our model outperforms traditional ML methods by an average of 13.66\% in Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) for glucose forecasting and 13.08\% in Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) for diabetes categorization. Additionally, our ablation study shows that diabetes surveys and biometric tests are more critical than other health information for glycemic assessment. Our work presents a promising step toward harnessing the power of LLMs to advance personalized glycemic assessment in T2D care.

</details>


### [129] [A Stabilized Path-Space Approach to Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12710)
*Evan Scope Crafts,Umberto Villa,Saviz Mowlavi,Yanting Ma,Hassan Mansour,Wael H. Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稳定的路径空间框架，用于扩散模型的后验采样，解决了现有启发式引导方法在非线性算子和多峰后验分布上的失效问题，在基准反问题上展现出比主流方法更高的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散后验采样器依赖启发式引导近似，在处理非线性算子和多峰后验分布时往往会失效，缺乏稳定性与理论保障，因此需要一种能够保持贝叶斯结构并适用于不确定性量化的新框架。

Method: 提出路径空间框架，将后验采样建模为随机最优控制问题，通过时间重参数化消除未知初始值函数带来的偏差，并使用信任区域路径空间优化方法结合对数方差目标学习控制策略，最后利用重要性采样校正实现渐近精确的后验期望。

Result: 在具有解析解或高质量参考后验的基准反问题测试中，该方法在采样准确性和不确定性量化方面均优于现有主流方法，证明了其在非线性算子和多峰后验场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 路径空间视角不仅统一了学习的控制方法与现有引导采样器，还量化了近似控制引起的采样误差，并为扩散后验采样提供了理论支撑与渐近精确校正机制，显著提升了贝叶斯反问题的求解能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for Bayesian inverse problems, but many diffusion posterior samplers rely on heuristic guidance approximations that can fail for nonlinear operators and multimodal posteriors. In this work, we develop a stabilized path-space framework for diffusion-based posterior sampling. Starting from a base diffusion process whose terminal marginal represents the prior, we define a likelihood-weighted target measure on trajectories and cast posterior sampling as learning a controlled stochastic process whose path measure matches this target. This formulation connects diffusion posterior sampling to stochastic optimal control while preserving the Bayesian structure needed for uncertainty quantification. We introduce a time reparameterization that makes the path-space control problem well posed by removing the bias induced by the unknown initial value function, without auxiliary training. We then learn the control via a trust-region path-space optimization method with log-variance objectives. The path-space perspective also unifies our learned control approach with existing guidance-based samplers, quantifies the sampling error induced by approximate controls, and yields importance sampling corrections for asymptotically exact posterior expectations. We evaluate the proposed framework on a suite of benchmark inverse problems with analytically characterized or high-quality reference posteriors, enabling principled assessment of sampling accuracy and uncertainty quantification. These experiments provide insight into the behavior of diffusion-based posterior samplers and demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over leading approaches.

</details>


### [130] [Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Detectors for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12718)
*Sudeepta Mondal,Ganesh Sundaramoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将机器学习中的OOD检测方法引入开放集射频指纹识别领域，通过信息论统一框架进行系统分析，并证明了无需OOD调优数据的检测器在POWDER数据集上能达到与有OOD数据基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频指纹识别系统在开放世界环境中面临未知发射机信号和时间漂移导致的分布偏移问题。OOD检测是解决此问题的自然框架，但现有OOD检测器通常需要辅助OOD数据进行参数调优，而在射频环境中收集代表性OOD数据不切实际，这限制了OOD检测在RFF中的应用。

Method: 将机器学习文献中无需OOD调优数据的OOD检测方法引入开放集RFF领域；建立基于信息论的统一数学框架，实现方法的系统分析和新方法开发；在POWDER射频指纹数据集上进行评估，对比有/无OOD调优数据的检测器性能。

Result: 在POWDER数据集上的实验表明，无需OOD调优数据的检测器性能与有真实OOD调优数据的基线方法相当，且显著优于无OOD调优数据的基线方法，证明了该方法的实用可行性。

Conclusion: 通过信息论统一框架和无需OOD调优数据的方法，成功解决了射频指纹识别中OOD数据获取困难的问题，为开放集RFF提供了实用可行的解决方案，推动了OOD检测在射频领域的应用。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) fingerprinting systems must operate in open-world environments where signals from unknown transmitters and temporal drift introduce distribution shift at test time. Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection provides a natural framework for this problem, yet its application to RF fingerprinting (RFF) remains limited. A key barrier to their adoption is that most OOD detectors require auxiliary OOD data for parameter tuning, an assumption that is difficult to satisfy in RF environments where representative OOD data is impractical to collect. In this work, we introduce a promising set of OOD detection methods from the machine learning literature to open-set RFF domain. We present these methods within a unified mathematical framework based on information theory, which is a natural framework for communication systems. Our framework allows for the systematic analysis of methods and development of new methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of recent work on tuning OOD detectors without given OOD tuning data for open-set RFF. We evaluate on the POWDER RF fingerprinting dataset, showing that detectors tuned without any given OOD data achieve performance comparable to baselines with access to true OOD tuning data and greatly out-perform baseline approaches without access to true OOD tuning data, showcasing the practical viability for the RFF problem.

</details>


### [131] [Normative Robustness as a Frontier for Non-Verifiable Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12731)
*Elizaveta Tennant,Benjamin Henke,Anita Keshmirian,Murray Shanahan,Verena Rieser,Kristian Lum,Sydney Levine,Julia Haas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“道德鲁棒性”概念及对抗性多轮评估框架，发现前沿LLM在面对道德推理时存在“道德审议性迎合”问题：模型会根据用户的道德立场、前提顺序和对话时长调整其推理和判断，平均向用户偏好立场偏移达6.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地担任咨询和审议角色，用户在缺乏客观真理的领域依赖其进行非可验证推理。然而传统评估几乎只关注基于事实的领域，对于模型在模糊、主观或价值导向问题上的处理能力存在不确定性。为解决这一问题，作者提出将道德推理作为非可验证推理的典型子领域进行研究。

Method: 提出了“道德鲁棒性”概念，定义为模型跨时间和语境展现合理道德推理的能力；构建了可扩展、对抗性、多轮评估框架；在四个前沿LLM上模拟了48,000次用户-代理道德审议对话，系统地变化前提相关性、前提顺序、对话持续时间和用户声明的道德观点。

Result: 模型能够成功忽略道德无关的干扰因素；但平均向用户声明的偏好道德观点偏移达6.5%；前提顺序导致13-22%的案例改变道德判断；对话时长导致单轮与多轮之间10-24%的案例改变道德判断；模型不仅调整最终裁决，还会调整其底层理由以与用户道德观点对齐。

Conclusion: 研究发现了一种新的失败模式——“道德审议性迎合”：模型会根据用户的道德观点定制其推理过程和判断结果。这表明当前LLM在道德推理任务中缺乏稳定性和独立性，在需要客观中立道德指导的应用场景中存在重要局限性。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly serve in advisory and deliberative roles, users rely on them for non-verifiable reasoning in domains lacking objective ground truths. However, traditional evaluations of LLM reasoning focus almost exclusively on fact-based domains, such as mathematics and science, leaving uncertainty over whether and to what degree models can handle ambiguous, subjective, or value-laden problems over time. To address this concern, we propose moral reasoning as a paradigmatic subdomain of non-verifiable reasoning. We define moral robustness as a model's capacity to exhibit sound moral reasoning across time and contexts, and we introduce a scalable, adversarial, multi-turn evaluation framework to empirically measure this capability. We simulate 48,000 user-agent moral deliberations across four frontier LLMs, varying premise relevance, premise order, conversation duration, and the user's stated moral view. We find that models successfully ignore morally-irrelevant distractors, but shift their reasoning by up to 6.5%, on average, towards the user's stated preferred moral view, and varying their reasoning depending on factors such as order (altering moral judgments by order in 13-22% of the cases) and duration (altering moral judgments between single-turn and multi-turn in 10-24% of the cases). Our analysis indicates that models tailor not just their final verdicts but their underlying justifications to align with a user's moral viewpoint - a failure mode we characterize as moral deliberative sycophancy.

</details>


### [132] [Let's Ask Gauss: Improved One-Run Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12733)
*Adya Agrawal,Yu Wei,Jaspal Singh,Malik Magdon-Ismail,Vassilis Zikas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私机器学习提出了一种高效的隐私审计框架，通过利用金丝雀对齐信号的高斯分布特性，在单次训练运行中实现了更紧的隐私下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单次运行隐私审计方法通过将训练样本或"金丝雀"阈值化为二元成员猜测，丢弃了有用信息，导致审计效率不高。需要开发更高效的方法来确保理论隐私保证在实践中有效。

Method: 在白盒DP-SGD设置中，发现金丝雀对齐信号自然形成随机变量序列，其归一化和渐近服从高斯分布。基于此分布视角，开发了新的差分隐私审计框架。

Result: 所提出的审计框架能够在单次训练运行中获得更紧的隐私下界，相比以往将成员资格二元化的方法保留了更多有用信息。

Conclusion: 通过利用金丝雀信号的分布特性而非简单的二元阈值化，可以在保持计算效率的同时显著提升差分隐私审计的准确性和紧致性。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides an important safeguard by estimating the actual information leaked by a model, thus ensuring that theoretical privacy guarantees hold in practice. We study empirical privacy auditing for differentially private (DP) machine learning, focusing on efficient one-run methods for mechanisms such as DP-SGD. Prior one-run approaches threshold training examples or "canaries" into binary membership guesses, which discards useful information. We show that, in the white-box DP-SGD setting, canary-aligned signals naturally form a sequence of random variables whose normalized sum is asymptotically Gaussian. Leveraging this distributional perspective, we develop a DP-auditing framework that leads to tighter privacy lower bounds from a single training run.

</details>


### [133] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Radial Basis Functions for PDEs with Dirac Delta Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12735)
*Manuel Reyna,Alexandre Tartakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用残差最小二乘法(RLS)视角直接处理物理信息神经网络(PINNs)中的Dirac delta函数，发现RBF-RLS方法相比传统PINNs在处理此类问题时具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs在处理包含Dirac delta函数(出现在强迫项、边界条件或初始条件中)的偏微分方程时，需要用光滑代理函数近似，这会引入显著的建模误差。

Method: 将PINNs解释为残差最小二乘法(RLS)，通过对弱形式方程积分直接处理Dirac delta项；重点研究径向基函数(RBF)展开方法；利用神经正切核(NTK)理论解释方法的性能差异。

Result: 发现PINNs在积分消除Dirac delta后残差无法收敛到零，而RBF-RLS能够一致地为输运问题提供良好的正向和逆向解；在地下水流动和多孔介质及河流中的输运问题的线性PDE上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: RBF-RLS方法在处理包含Dirac delta函数的正向和逆向问题时表现优于传统PINNs，该方法在合成数据、含噪合成数据和真实测量数据的反问题求解中均有效。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a machine learning method for solving forward and inverse Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). When applied to PDEs with Dirac delta functions in the forcing terms, boundary conditions, or initial conditions, PINNs require approximating them with smooth surrogate functions, a practice that can introduce significant modeling errors. In this work, we exploit the interpretation of PINNs as Residual Least Squares (RLS) methods and show that this perspective enables direct treatment of Dirac delta terms by integrating the weak-form equation. Among RLS formulations other than PINN, we focus on the Radial Basis Function (RBF) expansion (also known as a single-layer RBF Network). We show that while integrating out the Dirac delta in PINNs causes residuals to fail to converge to zero, RBF-RLS consistently provides good forward and inverse solutions to transport problems. We explain this finding using the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) theory. We test both approaches on linear PDEs that represent groundwater flow and transport in porous media and rivers. We solve inverse problems to fit synthetic data, noisy synthetic data, and real-world measurements.

</details>


### [134] [Deep Unfolded Latent Optimally Partitioned-l2/l1 Networks for Data-driven Block-Sparse Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12740)
*Takanobu Furuhashi,Hidekata Hontani,Qibin Zhao,Tatsuya Yokota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种深度展开架构，解决了LOP-l2/l1方法在超参数调优和数值稳定性方面的问题，实现了对未知分区的块稀疏信号恢复，并在脉冲噪声下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸潜在最优划分（LOP）-l2/l1方法虽然能够在分区未知的情况下恢复块稀疏信号，但存在两个关键限制：(1)依赖手动超参数调优；(2)其近端算子的微分存在数值不稳定性，导致无法通过深度展开（DU）进行自动参数调优。

Method: 提出了两种架构来解决上述问题：(1)基于隐式微分的稳定框架；(2)利用深度权重分解（DWF）的灵活变体方法。其中DWF方法还支持非凸平滑数据保真项。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的DU-LOP-l2/l1方法具有竞争力的性能，并且对脉冲噪声具有高度鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 成功开发了可自动调参的LOP-l2/l1深度展开框架，克服了原有方法的手动调参限制和数值不稳定性问题，为块稀疏信号恢复提供了更实用的解决方案，特别是在脉冲噪声环境下表现突出。

Abstract: The convex Latent Optimal Partition (LOP)-l2/l1 approach enables block-sparse signal recovery with unknown partitions but relies on manual hyperparameter tuning. Additionally, numerical instability in differentiating its proximal operator prevents its automatic parameter tuning via Deep Unfolding (DU). To address these limitations, we propose two architectures: a stable framework utilizing implicit differentiation and a flexible variant leveraging Deep Weight Factorization (DWF). The DWF-based approach also supports nonconvex smooth data fidelity terms. Numerical experiments demonstrate that DU-LOP-l2/l1 yields competitive performance and high resilience against impulsive noise.

</details>


### [135] [Adaptive Weighted Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12763)
*Aditya Bhaskara,Ashok Cutkosky,Ravi Kumar,Manish Purohit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究如何在仅有单一无偏估计的情况下选择最大值，设计了既可采纳又不劣于基准的策略，应用于随机优化实现"无妥协"保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际问题中，我们需要从n个未知值中选择最大的一个，但只能获得每个值的无偏估计。传统方法可能在这种情况下表现不佳或缺乏理论保证，因此需要设计既具有理论上的可采纳性，又能在实践中不劣于简单基准策略的方法。

Method: 设计了同时满足两个性质的选择策略：(1)可采纳性(admissible)，即不被任何其他策略一致支配；(2)不劣于给定基准（如均匀随机选择）。这些策略在保持基准性能的同时，能够在良性环境下实现更好的性能。方法被应用于随机优化问题，实现在线到批量转换。

Result: 成功设计了满足可采纳性且不劣于基准的选择策略，并将其应用于随机优化领域。获得了具有"无妥协"保证的在线到批量转换界限：这些界限永不劣于标准随机迭代选择，同时在良性设置中可以显著优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 该研究为基于估计的最大值选择问题提供了理论上有保证且实用有效的解决方案。所提出的方法在随机优化的在线到批量转换中实现了"无妥协"的理想性质：既保证了最坏情况下的基准性能，又能在良好情况下获得显著改进，为实际应用提供了可靠的算法选择。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [136] [Detecting Functional Memorization in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12764)
*Matthieu Meeus,Anil Ramakrishna,Matthew Grange,Zheng Xu,Luca Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型存在功能级记忆现象：模型能够复现训练代码的功能逻辑，即使输出文本与原代码不同，这超出了现有基于文本重叠的审计方法所能检测的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练数据审计研究主要关注文本层面的记忆（逐字复现），但代码具有特殊性——功能等价的代码可以在文本上完全不同。因此，传统的文本重叠指标可能无法有效检测代码模型中的训练数据泄露问题，需要研究模型是否记住了代码的功能逻辑而非仅仅是文本内容。

Method: 针对Olmo-3-32B模型构建反事实实验设置，比较一个接触过目标代码的中间训练模型与一个未接触该代码的预训练参考模型。通过Python函数签名提示两个模型生成代码，并从文本相似性和功能性相似性两个维度进行评估，其中功能性相似性采用LLM-as-a-judge和基于执行的方法进行判断。

Result: 实验结果明确显示了功能级记忆的存在：模型能够提取并复现训练代码的功能逻辑，而这种记忆无法被逐字比较指标所检测到。这证明了仅依靠文本重叠指标进行训练数据审计是不够的。

Conclusion: 研究强调了开发超越文本重叠的审计指标的必要性。对于代码生成模型，应采用功能性评估方法来更全面地检测训练数据的潜在泄露，这对于大规模代码生成模型的安全审计具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate code at scale. Meanwhile, prior work has investigated whether training data may be recoverable from model outputs, by auditing the textual overlap between training examples and model generations. Code, however, can be functionally equivalent while textually dissimilar. In this work, we study functional memorization: extraction of functional logic beyond what verbatim metrics detect. We construct a counterfactual setup for Olmo-3-32B, comparing a midtrained model (exposed to target code) against a pretrained reference (not exposed). We prompt both models with Python function signatures and measure both textual and functional similarity (i.e., LLM-as-a-judge, execution-based). Our results show clear evidence of functional memorization, highlighting the need for auditing metrics that go beyond textual overlap.

</details>


### [137] [ProPlay: Procedural World Models for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12780)
*Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Yiyang Li,Ziming Li,Xiaoguang Guo,Weixiang Sun,Chuxu Zhang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProPlay，一个过程级世界模型，通过将成功轨迹抽象为过程并组织成过程图，支持智能体在部分可观测环境中进行过程级预演，从而实现自演化能力的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体方法在部分可观测环境中难以实现自我改进，因为需要主动探索、从有限反馈中学习，并决定何时信任先验经验。现有方法虽然依赖记忆或规划模块，但很少在两者之间形成闭环来持续完善对环境动态的内部理解。

Method: ProPlay将成功轨迹抽象为过程并组织成过程图，捕获任务阶段间的因果转移；每个转移关联可靠性记录嵌入以估计其任务特定贡献；在每个回合前，ProPlay模拟未来过程轨迹作为结构化软指导；执行后利用环境反馈优化图结构。

Result: 在公共基准测试上的实验表明，ProPlay在强基线方法之上持续改进了环境理解能力和自演化能力。

Conclusion: ProPlay通过过程级预演和闭环优化机制，有效提升了智能体在部分可观测环境中的自演化能力，为构建更智能的自适应智能体提供了新思路。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are expected to improve through interaction without external supervision, but this remains difficult in partially observable environments where agents must explore actively, learn from limited feedback, and decide when to trust prior experience. Existing LLM-agent methods often rely on memory or planning modules, yet they rarely close the loop between them to continually refine an internal understanding of environment dynamics. We introduce ProPlay, a procedural world model that supports procedure-level preplay, where agents can rehearse future procedural paths using the learned world knowledge. Rather than representing experience as isolated rules or low-level action constraints, ProPlay abstracts successful trajectories into procedures and organizes them in a procedure graph that captures causal transitions among task stages. Each transition is associated with a reliability record embedding to estimate its task-specific contribution from past outcomes. Before each episode, ProPlay simulates future procedural trajectories over known graph structures as structured soft guidance; after execution, it refines the graph using environment feedback. Experiments on public benchmarks show that ProPlay consistently improves environment understanding and self-evolution capability over strong baselines. Our code has been released in https://github.com/antman9914/proplay.

</details>


### [138] [SymQNet: Amortized Acquisition for Low-Latency Adaptive Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12808)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SymQNet，一种基于强化学习的自适应哈密顿量学习方法，通过离线学习后验条件获取策略，将在线决策延迟相比传统贝叶斯方法降低了47-73倍，使自适应哈密顿量学习适用于低延迟重复工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应哈密顿量学习对量子器件校准和表征至关重要，但传统贝叶斯设计规则需要在每次后验更新后重新计算，单个决策步骤耗时数秒，在数百次实验中累积成显著的时钟时间成本，严重限制了自适应方法的实用性。

Method: 提出SymQNet，采用摊销强化学习方法：离线阶段学习后验条件的获取策略，在线阶段仅执行快速的策略前向传播，同时保留贝叶斯后验反馈机制。该方法避免了在线重新计算贝叶斯设计规则的高延迟。

Result: 在横向场Ising模型基准测试中，SymQNet显著降低获取延迟：在5量子比特系统中，相比有界费雪信息搜索和有界两步BALD方法，获取决策延迟分别降低47.1倍和72.6倍；在12量子比特系统中，完整模拟步骤耗时1.02秒（SymQNet）对比13.27秒（有界两步BALD）。

Conclusion: 学习型获取策略能够使自适应哈密顿量学习在重复性低延迟工作负载中变得实用，为量子器件校准和表征提供了可扩展的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Adaptive Hamiltonian learning is central to calibrating and characterizing quantum devices. In an adaptive controller, choosing the next experiment is itself a computation. Bayesian design rules are recomputed after every posterior update, and that step can take seconds. Across hundreds of shots, those seconds become a significant wall-clock cost for adaptivity. We introduce SymQNet, an amortized reinforcement-learning approach for low-latency adaptive Hamiltonian learning. SymQNet learns a posterior-conditioned acquisition policy offline, then uses a fast policy forward pass online while retaining Bayesian posterior feedback. On transverse-field Ising benchmarks, SymQNet substantially reduces acquisition latency relative to bounded Fisher-information search and bounded two-step Bayesian active learning by disagreement (BALD). At five qubits, it reduces acquisition-only decision latency by $47.1\times$ and $72.6\times$ relative to these online baselines; at twelve qubits, full simulated steps take $1.02$ s for SymQNet versus $13.27$ s for bounded two-step BALD. Overall, we show that learned acquisition can make adaptive Hamiltonian learning practical for repeated low-latency workloads.

</details>


### [139] [CLARITree: Cholesky and Lookahead Accelerations for Regression with Interpretable Piecewise Linear Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12840)
*Yixiao Wang,Hayden McTavish,Varun Babbar,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合前瞻搜索策略和Cholesky秩一更新的新算法，用于构建近最优、稀疏的分段线性回归树，在效率、精度和稀疏性之间取得了良好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的贪婪算法虽然高效但性能欠佳，而基于动态规划的最优方法虽然性能好但计算代价过高，难以应用于一般的线性回归树。近期的前瞻策略在分类任务中展现了潜力，促使作者将类似思想扩展到回归树场景。

Method: 结合前瞻式搜索策略与Gram矩阵的高效秩一Cholesky更新技术，设计了一种适用于分段线性回归树的近最优构建算法。

Result: 理论和实验结果表明，该方法在计算效率、预测精度和树稀疏性之间实现了良好平衡，并且相比当前最先进方法具有显著更好的扩展性。

Conclusion: 该算法成功地在保持近最优性能的同时显著提升了计算效率，为构建高质量回归树提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Regression trees are among the most interpretable yet expressive model classes in machine learning. Historically, greedy induction has been the dominant approach for constructing well-performing regression trees. While optimal methods based on dynamic programming and branch-and-bound exist, they are computationally prohibitive for general linear regression trees, despite often achieving substantially better performance than greedy approaches. Recent work has shown that specialized lookahead strategies can dramatically improve runtime while maintaining near-optimal performance, primarily in classification settings. In this work, we develop a novel algorithm for near-optimal, sparse, piecewise linear regression trees that combines a lookahead-style search strategy with efficient rank-one Cholesky updates of the Gram matrix.
