Joint coverage and renewable optimization reduces daily grid consumption by 28.5\% with 32 solar panels at the diminishing-returns threshold, with 17 base stations identified as both coverage-active and high-priority solar candidates as evidence that cross-domain NDT coordination enables sustainable, intent-driven 6G network management.

</details>


### [214] [Revolutionizing Wireless Communications with Space Data Centers: Applications and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13086)
*Minghao Sun,Zehui Chen,Jinbo Hou,Kezhi Wang,Xiaoli Chu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了空间数据中心（SDCs）如何将空间通信范式从面向连接的数据传输转变为面向任务和服务为中心的信息交换，并提出了分层网络架构和部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着未来AI产业的发展，传统的卫星主要作为中继节点或轻量级星载处理器，已无法满足数据密集型和智能驱动的空间应用需求。空间数据中心（SDCs）作为一种新兴的轨道计算基础设施，集成了通信、计算、存储和控制能力，因此需要研究其如何变革空间通信范式。

Method: 文章首先提出了包含接入层、中继层、计算层和控制层的分层SDC网络架构，并概述了可能的部署策略。随后探讨了SDCs支持的未来应用场景，分析了其通信特征及相关研究挑战。通过仿真实验验证了SDCs在降低分层空间网络控制层延迟方面的有效性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，SDCs能够有效降低分层空间网络中控制层的延迟，验证了其在提升空间网络性能方面的潜力。文章还识别出了面向实际部署的关键研究方向。

Conclusion: 空间数据中心（SDCs）作为一种新兴轨道计算基础设施，能够推动空间通信范式向服务为中心的方向转变。未来需要在网络架构、部署策略以及关键技术方面进行深入研究，以实现SDCs的实际部署。

Abstract: Space data centers (SDCs) are emerging as a promising orbital computing infrastructure for the future AI industry. Unlike conventional satellites that mainly serve as relay nodes or lightweight onboard processors, SDCs integrate communication, computing, storage, and control capabilities in orbit, enabling persistent service support for data-intensive and intelligence-driven space applications. In this article, we investigate how SDCs may transform space communication paradigms from connectivity-oriented data transmission toward task-oriented and service-centric information exchange. We first present a hierarchical SDC network architecture consisting of access, relay, computing, and control layers, and outline possible deployment strategies. We then explore representative future application scenarios enabled by SDCs, highlighting their communication characteristics and associated research challenges. Simulation results further demonstrate the effectiveness of SDCs in reducing control-layer latency in hierarchical space networks. Finally, we identify key research directions toward the practical deployment of SDCs.

</details>


### [215] [Feasibility Assessment of Remote Driving via Latency Analysis of ITS-G5 and Cellular Networks in the MASA Living Lab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13292)
*Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Antonio Solida,Salvatore Iandolo,Marco Savarese,Martin Klapez,Enrico Rossini,Marcello Pietri,Marco Picone,Marco Mamei,Maurizio Casoni,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在城市级真实测试床MASA中对比评估了ITS-G5和5G蜂窝网络在远程驾驶场景下的时延性能，分析了不同网络负载下的端到端时延特性，为混合通信策略提供了实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶是网联自动驾驶的关键使能技术，但其实际部署依赖于无线网络能够保证低且可预测的时延。然而，目前缺乏在真实城市环境中对ITS-G5和蜂窝网络时延性能的系统评估和对比研究。

Method: 在Modena Automotive Smart Area (MASA)城市级测试床上进行受控实验，在不同网络负载和交通条件下测量ITS-G5和5G技术的网络时延和端到端时延组件。测试场景包括：车辆到远程操作员的上行视频流传输，以及远程操作员到车辆的控制命令下行链路。

Result: 实验测量揭示了两种技术在时延和可变性方面的差异，以及基础设施覆盖对视频流传输性能的影响。基于观测到的时延分布和可靠性指标，评估了在混合网络环境中远程驾驶的实际可行性和安全裕度。

Conclusion: 研究结果为未来远程操作部署提供了可操作的见解，并提出了结合ITS-G5和蜂窝网络优势的混合通信策略，以提升远程驾驶的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Remote driving has gained increasing attention as a key enabler for connected and automated vehicles. Yet its practical deployment hinges on wireless networks' ability to guarantee low, predictable latency. In this paper, we present an extensive latency analysis of ITS-G5 and cellular (5G) technologies within the Modena Automotive Smart Area (MASA), a real-world, city-scale testbed equipped with a distributed intelligent transportation infrastructure. By conducting controlled experiments under varying network loads and traffic conditions, we measure network and end-to-end latency components relevant to remote driving, in which the uplink consists of a continuous video stream transmitted from the vehicle to the remote operator, and the downlink conveys control commands back to the car. Measurements conducted under diverse conditions reveal how latency and variability differ across the two technologies and how infrastructure coverage impacts video-stream transmission performance. Based on the observed latency distributions and reliability metrics, we assess the practical feasibility and safety margins of remote driving in mixed network environments. The results provide actionable insights for future teleoperation deployments and motivate hybrid communication strategies that combine the strengths of ITS-G5 and cellular networks.

</details>


### [216] [Measurement-Based Performance Evaluation of SmartRSUs with Heterogeneous Antenna Architectures for V2X Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13334)
*Marco Savarese,Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Salvatore Iandolo,Antonio Solida Martin Klapez,Maurizio Casoni,Micaela Verucchi,Enrico Vincenzi,Ignacio Sanudo Olmedo,Marko Bertogna,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对比评估了两种不同天线架构的SmartRSU性能，发现天线设计与放置对V2X通信可靠性和延迟有显著影响，为C-ITS系统中SmartRSU的优化设计提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着协作智能交通系统(C-ITS)的发展，路侧单元(RSU)的通信性能对系统可靠性至关重要。然而，不同天线架构设计对V2X通信性能的实际影响尚缺乏基于实测的客观评估，这限制了下一代SmartRSU的优化设计。

Method: 构建两种定制SmartRSU：一种采用集成GNSS与通信天线的车顶模块，另一种使用外部双ITS-G5天线(5.9GHz)和专用GNSS天线。基于专有OBU平台，在真实环境中评估覆盖范围、RSSI、丢包率和端到端延迟等关键V2X指标。通过同址部署商用V2X RSU作为基准参考，确保在相同环境条件下的客观对比验证。

Result: 实验结果揭示了集成天线与外部天线配置在实际部署场景中的性能差异与权衡关系。天线设计和放置策略显著影响通信可靠性和延迟性能，不同架构在覆盖范围、信号强度和传输稳定性方面呈现不同特性。

Conclusion: 本研究为下一代SmartRSU的设计和优化提供了重要的实践见解，证明了天线架构选择需要根据具体应用场景和性能需求进行权衡，为C-ITS基础设施部署提供了有价值的参考依据。

Abstract: This paper presents a measurement-based performance evaluation of two custom Smart Roadside Units (SmartRSUs) featuring different V2X antenna architectures. The first configuration integrates GNSS and communication antennas into an all-in-one rooftop module, whereas the second uses external dual ITS-G5 (IEEE 802.11p) antennas operating at 5.9~GHz and a dedicated GNSS antenna. Both systems are built upon a proprietary On-Board Unit (OBU) platform adapted for infrastructure deployment.
  The experimental campaign evaluates key V2X communication metrics, including coverage, received signal strength indicator (RSSI), packet loss, and end-to-end latency in both transmission (OBU-to-infrastructure) and reception (infrastructure-to-OBU) directions. To ensure objective validation, a commercial off-the-shelf V2X Roadside Unit is co-located on the same infrastructure and used as a performance benchmark, providing ground-truth reference measurements under identical environmental conditions through a controlled co-located deployment.
  Results highlight the impact of antenna design and placement on communication reliability and latency, revealing trade-offs between integrated and external antenna configurations in real-world deployment scenarios. The findings provide practical insights for the design and optimization of next-generation SmartRSUs in cooperative intelligent transportation systems (C-ITS).

</details>


### [217] [Experimental Insights into UDP-Based Video and Control Traffic over IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13390)
*Antonio Solida,Gaetano Orazio Cauchi,Salvatore Iandolo,Marco Savarese,Martin Klapez,Maurizio Casoni,Carlo Augusto Grazia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验评估了IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5测试平台上UDP传输的性能，发现调制方案是低负载下影响延迟的主导因素，而传输模式和IP版本在高负载下更为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆应用（如协同驾驶、远程操作和实时感知）日益依赖低延迟无线通信。ITS-G5作为车对车和车对基础设施直接通信的关键技术，但在现实条件下针对UDP流量的实验研究仍然有限。

Method: 在由Raspberry Pi车载单元和商用路侧单元组成的IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5测试床上进行实验评估。研究不同调制编码方案(MCS)的影响，评估两种网络层配置（IPv4单播和IPv6多播），以及CAKE主动队列管理的使用。测试包括iPerf生成的合成流量和使用MPEG-TS over UDP的实时视频流。

Result: 调制方案是低流量负载下影响延迟的主导因素，而在拥塞条件下传输模式和IP版本的选择变得更加重要。高阶调制显著降低了延迟和变异性，IPv6多播比IPv4单播表现出更大的延迟分散。主动队列管理似乎并不能改善延迟可预测性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为配置支持延迟敏感车辆服务的ITS-G5网络提供了实用的见解，有助于优化车载通信系统的性能配置。

Abstract: Vehicular applications such as cooperative driving, teleoperation, and real-time perception increasingly rely on low-latency wireless communication. In this context, ITS-G5, based on IEEE 802.11p, represents a key technology for enabling direct vehicle-to-vehicle and vehicle-to-infrastructure communication. Despite its relevance, experimental studies focusing on the performance of UDP-based traffic over IEEE 802.11p under realistic conditions remain limited.
  This paper presents an experimental evaluation of UDP transmission over an IEEE 802.11p ITS-G5 testbed composed of Raspberry Pi-based onboard units and commercial roadside units. The analysis investigates the impact of different modulation and coding schemes (MCS). It also evaluates two network-layer configurations (IPv4 unicast and IPv6 multicast) and the use of CAKE for active queue management. In addition to synthetic traffic generated with iPerf, the evaluation includes real-time video streaming using MPEG-TS over UDP to emulate latency-sensitive vehicular applications. Results show that the modulation scheme is the dominant factor influencing latency at low traffic loads, while the choice of transmission mode and IP version becomes increasingly significant under congested conditions. Higher-order modulations significantly reduce latency and variability, whereas IPv6 multicast exhibits greater delay dispersion than IPv4 unicast. Furthermore, active queue management does not seem to improve delay predictability. These findings provide practical insights for configuring ITS-G5 networks supporting latency-sensitive vehicular services.

</details>


### [218] [From Traditional Automation to Embodied Wireless Intelligence: Vision-Language-Action Empowered Physics-Aware Communication Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13458)
*Genze Jiang,Kezhi Wang,Xiaomin Chen,Yizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出具身智能赋能基站(eBS)范式，通过视觉-语言-动作(VLA)管道将基站转变为自主智能体，实现环境感知、因果推理和物理感知的零样本自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线网络自动化系统（从基于规则的自组织网络到数据驱动优化）本质上是离身的，仅优化性能指标而缺乏对控制无线电波传播的物理环境的感知能力。

Method: 提出具身智能赋能基站(eBS)，采用双层异步架构：语义规划器利用前沿视觉-语言模型(VLM)在人类时间尺度生成结构化动作指令；战术控制器执行实时自适应。整体通过视觉-语言-动作(VLA)管道实现。

Result: 案例研究表明，单一VLA管道无需任务特定训练即可实现零样本材质推理、跨视点泛化，并能在信号恶化前预测动态事件。

Conclusion: 展示了从传统遵循规则的网络自动化向具身智能赋能的未来无线网络的范式转变。

Abstract: Wireless network automation has progressed from rule-based self-organising networks (SON) to data-driven optimisation, yet existing systems remain fundamentally disembodied, optimising performance indicators without perceiving the physical environment that governs radio propagation. We propose the embodied intelligent empowered base station (eBS), a paradigm that adopts a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) pipeline to transform base stations into autonomous agents capable of situated perception, causal physical reasoning, and physics-aware action generation. The eBS employs a two-tier asynchronous architecture: a Semantic Planner powered by a frontier Vision-Language Model (VLM) generates structured action directives on human timescales, whilst a Tactical Controller executes real-time adaptation. Case studies demonstrate that a single VLA pipeline, without task-specific training, can perform zero-shot material reasoning, generalise across viewpoints, and predict dynamic events before signal degradation occurs, illustrating a paradigm shift from traditional rule-following network automation to embodied intelligence empowered future wireless networks.

</details>


### [219] [Graphical Causal Reasoning for Root Cause Analysis in Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13532)
*Fabien Chraim,Dominik Janzing,John Evans*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图因果发现的云网络事件根因分析方法，在实际生产环境中对800多起事件有效，准确率达85.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算依赖的大规模网络是复杂的系统，传统的基于规则的自动化根因分析存在局限性，需要一种更有效的数据驱动方法来处理云网络事件的根因分析问题。

Method: 引入时空分组策略和自动化本体论来降低问题维度；使用双变量格兰杰因果关系和条件独立性测试从二值时间序列数据构建因果图；提出一种概率方法，分配边特定的条件概率作为时间滞后的函数，通过因果图遍历实现可解释的、时间感知的根因评分。

Result: 在来自主要云提供商的35个生产事件标记数据集上评估，模型在85.7%的事件中成功召回正确根因，74.3%产生精确匹配；在生产环境中已用于800多起真实事件，获得网络工程师的积极定性反馈。

Conclusion: 基于数据驱动的因果方法在动态大规模运营环境中进行根因分析具有实用性，能够有效解决云网络事件的根因分析问题。

Abstract: Cloud-computing relies on large-scale networks which are inherently complex systems. In this paper, we present a novel approach to root cause analysis (RCA) of cloud network incidents, leveraging graph-based causal discovery techniques. Our method addresses the limitations of rule-based automation by introducing a spatiotemporal grouping strategy and an automation ontology to reduce the dimensionality of the problem. We construct a causal graph from binary time series data using bivariate Granger causality and conditional independence tests. For inference, we introduce a probabilistic method that assigns edge-specific conditional probabilities as a function of time lag, allowing for interpretable, time-aware root cause scoring via causal graph traversal.
  We evaluated the system using a labeled dataset of 35 production incidents from a major cloud provider. The model successfully recalled the correct root cause in 85.7% of incidents and produced an exact match in 74.3%. In production, the deployed system has been used in over 800 real-world incidents, with positive qualitative feedback from network engineers. These results highlight the practicality of a data-driven, causal approach to RCA in dynamic and large-scale operational environments.

</details>


### [220] [NetCause: Counterfactual Learning for Root Cause Analysis in Large-Scale Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13543)
*Fabien Chraim,Jian Zhang,Dominik Janzing,Xiang Song,Christos Faloutsos,John Evans*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NetCause是一个自监督学习框架，通过图时序建模和反事实模拟对网络故障进行根因排序，在真实云网络中相比规则基线准确率提升16.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析技术依赖静态规则、相关性启发式方法或局部拓扑推理，在故障跨越复杂物理和逻辑依赖传播的动态环境中难以泛化，无法准确捕获故障在大规模网络中的传播机制并进行因果归因。

Method: 提出NetCause框架，将网络事件建模为图时序过程，采用自监督学习方式，通过反事实模拟对候选根因进行排序，生成可解释的根因假设排名，并自然集成运营商定义的缓解和修复操作。

Result: 在某领先云服务商生产网络上收集的1,500+起事件（6个月）中训练，在31个专家标注事件上评估。在最相关的运营决策场景中，根因排序质量显著提升，相比基于规则的启发式基线准确率提高16.1%。推理轻量化，每个事件仅需数秒GPU运行时间（远低于典型遥测收集延迟）。

Conclusion: 基于学习的图时序建模与反事实模拟方法能够有效捕获故障传播机制，实现准确的根因因果归因，在真实大规模云网络环境中具有实用价值，虽然训练计算密集，但推理效率满足在线运营需求。

Abstract: Can a learned model capture how faults propagate through a large-scale network and use this knowledge to causally attribute customer impact to its underlying root cause? Existing root cause analysis techniques often rely on static rules, correlation heuristics, or topology-local reasoning, which struggle to generalize in dynamic environments where faults propagate across complex physical and logical dependencies.
  We present NetCause, a self-supervised learning-based framework that models network incidents as graph-temporal processes and uses counterfactual simulation to rank candidate root causes. This approach produces an interpretable ranking of root cause hypotheses and integrates naturally with operator-defined mitigation and remediation actions.
  We train the model on over 1,500 incidents collected over six months from a leading cloud provider's production network and evaluate it on 31 expert-labeled incidents. NetCause consistently improves root cause ranking quality in the regime most relevant to operational decision-making, achieving a 16.1% accuracy improvement over a rule-based heuristic baseline. While training is computationally intensive, inference is lightweight, requiring only seconds of GPU runtime per incident (well below typical telemetry collection latencies).

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [ITME: Inference Tiered Memory Expansion with Disaggregated CXL-Hybrid Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12556)
*Hakbeom Jang,Younghoon Min,Sunwoong Kim,Taeyoung Ahn,Hanyee Kim,Youngpyo Joo,Hoshik Kim,Jongryool Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ITME系统，利用CXL混合内存技术实现TB级字节可寻址远程内存扩展，在保持成本效益的同时，将系统吞吐量提升了最高35.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型向智能体和长上下文工作负载演进，推理所需的上下文状态规模已达TB级别，超出单服务器承载能力。现有基于JBOF架构的NVMe-oF方案仍存在软件优化复杂、成本高昂等问题，亟需一种新型架构以高效扩展共享上下文基础设施。

Method: 提出ITME（Inference Tiered Memory Expansion）架构，采用CXL混合内存构建大规模字节可寻址远程内存扩展层。该方案的核心洞察是：大规模模型权重和前缀缓存具有确定性访问模式，系统可据此主动管理跨内存-存储层级的数据迁移，实现简化的软件栈与高效的内存扩展。研究团队使用商用级SK Hynix CMM模块和PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSD进行性能评估，并通过FPGA硬件原型验证功能可行性。

Result: ITME通过提供远程内存扩展以容纳超出主机内存限制的大型KV缓存占用，相比传统CPU卸载方案实现最高35.7%的吞吐量提升。实验证明了该架构在功能可行性和性能潜力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ITME成功解决了共享上下文基础设施的扩展挑战，为TB级上下文状态处理提供了成本高效且软件栈简化的解决方案。通过CXL技术的字节可寻址特性与确定性访问模式的主动数据管理，该架构为大语言模型的分布式推理基础设施提供了新的发展方向。

Abstract: The rapid shift toward agentic and long-context workloads in Large Language Models (LLMs) is pushing the industry beyond the capacity of individual servers toward disaggregated shared storage to handle TB-scale context states. This movement has led to the emergence of specialized shared context layers designed to externalize and share cumulative inference states across distributed clusters. While offloading to a data processing unit (DPU) within just-a-bunch-of-flash (JBOF) architectures accelerates NVMe-over-fabrics (NVMe-oF) target processing, the need for sophisticated software-level optimization and cost-efficiency burdens remain significant. Consequently, the ideal architecture for scaling this shared context infrastructure is still an active area of exploration. In this paper, we propose ITME (Inference Tiered Memory Expansion), which leverages a CXL-hybrid memory to present a massive, TB-scale byte-addressable remote memory expansion. This approach enables cost-efficient scaling and simplifies the software stack through direct byte-addressability, effectively addressing the challenges of shared context infrastructure. Our key insight is that the deterministic access patterns of voluminous model weights and prefix caches enable the system to proactively manage data movement across the memory-storage hierarchy. We validate ITME by evaluating its performance potential with production-grade SK Hynix CMM and PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSDs, while further demonstrating its functional feasibility through an FPGA-based hardware prototype. Overall, ITME enhances conventional CPU-offloading by providing additional remote memory expansion to accommodate large KV cache footprints beyond host memory limits, achieving up to a 35.7\% throughput improvement.

</details>


### [222] [Eidola: Modeling Multi-GPU Network Communication Traffic in Distributed AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12638)
*Ranganath R. Selagamsetty,Matthew Poremba,Bradford M. Beckmann,Joshua San Miguel,Mikko H. Lipasti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Eidola，一个基于gem5的可扩展仿真框架扩展，通过使用精简的GPU模型（eidolon）和真实应用的带注释时序配置文件，实现了大规模多GPU系统中核间通信流量的周期级精确建模与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式AI训练规模的扩大，多GPU系统成为主流。现有的内核融合和通信计算重叠等优化技术虽然减少了延迟，但引入了不规则且瞬态的流量模式，且严重依赖细粒度同步与点对点通信，给互连带宽和延迟带来巨大压力。现有工具难以对这些复杂的通信行为进行精确建模。

Method: 在gem5仿真框架上开发可扩展扩展Eidola。其核心设计是使用一个精简的GPU模型（eidolon），仅模拟流量建模所需的最小特征，从而实现可扩展性。系统利用真实应用程序的带注释时序配置文件，以周期级精度模拟点对点GPU写入，支持可配置的每GPU流量模式，并允许在不同通信场景下进行隔离的性能分析。

Result: 通过复现融合内核执行中的变异性以及实现一个SyncMon启发的同步机制，验证了Eidola的有效性。实验结果表明，该框架能够成功模拟同步行为，并确认了轮询相关内存流量的减少，证明了其在模拟和分析大规模多GPU配置下的同步行为方面的能力。

Conclusion: Eidola为研究GPU间通信提供了一个灵活且可扩展的平台，能够支持现代分布式GPU系统中的架构探索，帮助研究人员理解和优化复杂的多GPU通信场景。

Abstract: As distributed AI workloads grow in scale, multi-GPU systems have become essential for training large models. Although techniques like kernel fusion and overlapping communication with computation help reduce delays, they also introduce irregular and transient traffic patterns that are difficult to model using existing tools. These techniques rely heavily on fine-grained synchronization and peer-to-peer communication, which place significant pressure on interconnect bandwidth and latency.
  In this work, we introduce Eidola, a scalable extension to the gem5 simulation framework that enables detailed modeling of inter-GPU communication traffic. The extension is scalable as our GPU model serves as a succinct eidolon, emulating the minimal characteristics needed for traffic modeling. Eidola uses annotated timing profiles from real applications to emulate peer-to-peer GPU writes with cycle-level precision. This allows researchers to simulate and analyze synchronization behavior across large multi-GPU configurations. The simulator supports configurable per-GPU traffic patterns and enables isolated performance analysis under different communication scenarios.
  We demonstrate Eidola's effectiveness by reproducing variability in fused kernel execution and by implementing a SyncMon-inspired synchronization mechanism, confirming reductions in polling-related memory traffic. Our results show that Eidola provides a flexible and scalable platform for studying inter-GPU communication and supports architectural exploration in modern distributed GPU systems.

</details>


### [223] [On the Limits of Performance Portability in Directive-Based GPU Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12753)
*Alessandro Romeo,Nitin Shukla,Stefano Truzzi,Alessio Suriano,Andrea Mignone*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过将天体物理磁流体动力学代码gPLUTO从OpenACC移植到OpenMP，评估了基于指令的GPU编程的性能可移植性。在NVIDIA A100上两者性能相当，但在AMD MI250X上OpenMP实现比NVIDIA基线慢约3倍，内核级慢达47倍，主要受跨步内存访问模式和编译器限制影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用向GPU加速的百亿级系统过渡受到性能、可移植性和生产力之间权衡的制约。需要评估基于指令的GPU编程模型在不同硬件架构上的性能可移植性表现。

Method: 将生产级磁流体动力学代码gPLUTO从OpenACC移植到OpenMP，并在NVIDIA A100（Leonardo Booster）和AMD MI250X（LUMI-G）设备上进行性能分析和对比。通过内核级性能分析识别性能瓶颈。

Result: 在NVIDIA平台上，OpenACC和OpenMP由于共享编译器后端而达到可比性能。在AMD MI250X上，相同的OpenMP实现应用级性能比NVIDIA A100 OpenACC基线慢约3倍，内核级慢达一个数量级。性能瓶颈主要由跨步内存访问模式敏感性和编译器限制驱动，内存延迟限制而非峰值带宽是主要因素。低并行度内核中C++抽象层增加寄存器压力和溢出，导致特定情况下高达47倍的极端性能下降。

Conclusion: 跨GPU架构的可移植性能不仅需要应用级更改，还需要编译器后端的持续改进和架构感知的优化策略。性能可移植性的实现需要在多个层面协同优化。

Abstract: The transition of scientific applications to GPU-accelerated exascale systems is constrained by trade-offs between performance, portability, and productivity. This work evaluates the performance portability of directive-based GPU programming by porting gPLUTO, a production-grade magnetohydrodynamics code for astrophysical simulations, from OpenACC to OpenMP, and analyzing its performance on NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) and AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) devices. On NVIDIA platforms, OpenACC and OpenMP achieve comparable performance due to a shared compiler backend, providing a consistent baseline for assessing algorithmic efficiency. In contrast, the same OpenMP implementation is approximately three times slower at the application level on AMD MI250X with respect to the NVIDIA A100 OpenACC baseline, with kernel-level slowdowns reaching up to an order of magnitude, driven by sensitivity to strided memory-access patterns and compiler limitations. Kernel-level profiling shows that the dominant contributors to run-time are memory-latency-bound rather than limited by peak band-width. In low-parallelism kernels, C++ abstraction layers increase register pressure and spilling, leading to extreme slowdowns of up to 47x in specific cases. These results indicate that portable performance across GPU architectures requires not only application-level changes but also continued advances in compiler backends and architecture-aware optimization strategies

</details>


### [224] [SemanticXR: Low Power and Real-time Queryable Semantic Mapping with an Object-Level Device-Cloud Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12849)
*Rahul Singh,Devdeep Ray,Connor Smith,Sarita Adve*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SemanticXR是首个设备-云端协同的实时开放词汇语义映射系统，通过对象级优化在满足XR设备功耗、带宽和内存约束下实现了2.2倍延迟降低和毫秒级查询响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义映射方法计算密集且依赖服务器级资源，难以满足移动XR设备对开放词汇、实时和低功耗的需求。虽然云端卸载提供可行路径，但现有系统未能在设备-云端边界进行语义映射的合理分割或有效管理其通信、执行和内存开销。

Method: 提出SemanticXR系统，核心创新是将语义可识别对象提升为设备与服务器间通信、执行和内存的一等单元。服务器端采用对象级并行化和几何下采样降低映射延迟，对象级深度映射协同设计减少上行带宽；设备端采用对象级稀疏局部地图配合增量更新和优先级机制，实现网络鲁棒查询并控制内存和下行带宽。同时提供对象级可配置的资源与质量权衡机制。

Result: 在相同感知模型下，对象级组织使服务器端映射延迟降低2.2倍，语义质量保持不变；深度映射协同设计将上行带宽控制在2.5 Mbps以下；设备端在支持万个对象时查询延迟低于100 ms，网络中断时仍能保持性能，内存占用控制在500 MB内，支持数万个对象；下行带宽随地图变化量而非场景总规模扩展；正常操作时系统仅增加2%的设备功耗。

Conclusion: SemanticXR首次实现了设备-云端协同的实时开放词汇语义映射，证明了对象级抽象是解决XR设备资源约束的有效途径，为未来XR应用提供了可扩展、高效的语义理解基础设施。

Abstract: Semantic mapping is a core service that enables grounded interactions in emerging Extended Reality (XR) applications such as AI assistants and spatial object search. Deploying this capability on mobile XR devices requires a system that is open-vocabulary, real-time, and low-power. Existing approaches are compute-intensive and assume server-class resources. Cloud offloading offers a practical path, but no existing system splits semantic mapping across the device-cloud boundary or manages its communication, execution, and memory footprint.
  We present SemanticXR, the first device-cloud system for real-time, open-vocabulary semantic mapping and querying under XR power, bandwidth, and memory constraints. Our key insight is to elevate semantically identifiable objects to first-class units of communication, execution, and memory across the device and server. On the server, object-level parallelism and geometry downsampling improve mapping latency, while object-level depth-mapping co-design reduces upstream bandwidth. On the device, an object-level sparse local map with incremental updates and update prioritization enables network-robust querying with bounded memory and downstream bandwidth. Object-level configurable resource usage vs. quality trade-offs let applications and the system adapt mapping to application requirements and operating conditions, respectively.
  Against a device-cloud baseline with the same perception models, object-level organization improves server-side mapping latency by 2.2X at equal semantic quality. Depth-mapping co-design maintains upstream bandwidth under 2.5 Mbps. On the device, SemanticXR sustains sub-100 ms query latency for up to 10,000 objects even under network drops, supports tens of thousands of objects within 500 MB, and scales downstream bandwidth with map changes, not total scene size. The system adds only 2% device power during normal operation.

</details>


### [225] [High-Order Spectral Element Methods for Wave Propagation on ARM Multicore CPU with SME: Optimizations and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12850)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Wubing Wan,Zekun Yin,Wenqiang Wang,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对ARM处理器上的SME扩展优化了SPECFEM3D谱元法波传播模拟，实现了4-6倍性能提升，并发现SME改变了算法的离散化权衡策略，使得更高阶多项式更为有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于谱元法(SEM)的波传播模拟是典型的高性能计算负载，但现有SEM实现无法很好地适配新兴的支持可扩展矩阵扩展(SME)的ARM多核CPU架构，存在架构适配性问题。

Method: 在LX2处理器上实现了SME使能的SPECFEM3D优化，包含三个关键技术：(1)针对SEM张量积算子的SME感知批量小矩阵核；(2)面向有限HBM系统的内存感知混合MPI+OpenMP执行方案；(3)基于色散的(h,p)权衡等精度研究。

Result: 在固定多项式阶数条件下，优化实现相比原始代码实现了4-6倍全应用性能提升，相比优化的非SME CPU基线也有明显性能增益。更重要的是，SME将性能优势的运行点推向了基于色散等精度前沿的更高多项式阶数方向，进一步缩短了求解时间并减少了工作集大小。

Conclusion: SME不仅影响核函数效率，还改变了现代ARM多核平台上SEM的实际离散化权衡，表明硬件特性对算法选择和数值方法策略具有重要影响，为未来ARM HPC平台的SEM应用优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Wave propagation based on the spectral element method (SEM) is a representative HPC workload, but existing SEM implementations are not well matched to emerging ARM multicore CPUs with Scalable Matrix Extension (SME). We present an SME-enabled optimization of \textsc{SPECFEM3D} on the emerging LX2 processor that combines an SME-aware batched small-matrix kernel for SEM tensor-product operators, a memory-aware hybrid MPI+OpenMP execution scheme for limited-HBM systems, and a dispersion-based iso-accuracy study of the $(h,p)$ tradeoff. At fixed polynomial order, the optimized implementation improves full-application performance by 4--6$\times$ over the original code and delivers clear gains over optimized non-SME CPU baselines. Beyond these implementation-level gains, our results suggest that SME shifts the performance-favorable operating point toward higher polynomial orders along the dispersion-based iso-accuracy frontier, further reducing time-to-solution and working-set size. These results indicate that SME affects not only kernel efficiency, but also the practical discretization tradeoff for SEM on modern ARM multicore platforms.

</details>


### [226] [Maestro: Workload-Aware Cross-Cluster Scheduling for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12950)
*Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Yihui Zhang,Yilong Li,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Maestro，一个面向大语言模型多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）的负载感知调度系统，通过利用智能体语义信息进行分层调度，有效解决了资源受限环境下的GPU资源管理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）已成为解决复杂任务的强大范式，但在规模化部署时面临严峻系统挑战：每个用户查询会产生迭代式LLM调用流水线，资源消耗远超单轮查询；在资源受限的云环境中，这些工作流面临解码阶段的非确定性和输入相关成本、内存碎片化和过度配置的重尾多模型需求，以及跨集群调度权衡等问题。

Method: Maestro是一个基于智能体语义和角色的负载感知调度系统：(1)预测每个阶段的输出长度和内存使用量；(2)节点层面通过分层权重缓存和弹性内存供给实现动态多模型共置；(3)集群层面执行延迟感知路由以避免冷启动延迟和内存过载；(4)全局层面实施工作流感知优先级排序，最小化交互任务队首阻塞。

Result: 通过原型实验和轨迹驱动仿真验证，Maestro将KV-reservation HBM减少67.2%，在高竞争场景下的SLO达成率相比EDF提升23.6个百分点。

Conclusion: Maestro通过显式利用智能体语义信息驱动的分层调度策略，有效解决了LLM-MAS在严格GPU预算约束下的服务问题，显著降低了内存占用并提升了服务水平目标达成率。

Abstract: Large Language Model based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for tackling complex tasks by breaking them into collaborative workflows of specialized LLM-powered agents. However, deploying such multi-agent workloads at scale poses significant system challenges. Each user query spawns an iterative pipeline of LLM calls, greatly amplifying resource consumption compared to single-turn queries. In resource-constrained cloud settings, these workflows face non-deterministic and input-dependent costs at decode stage, heavy-tailed multi-model requirements with memory fragmentation and over-provisioning, and cross-cluster scheduling trade-offs. We present Maestro, a workload-aware scheduling system designed for LLM-MAS serving under strict GPU budgets. Maestro explicitly leverages agent semantics and roles: it predicts the output length and memory usage of each stage and uses this prediction to drive a hierarchical scheduler. At the node level, Maestro enables dynamic multi-model co-location via hierarchical weight caching and elastic memory provisioning. At the cluster level, it performs latency-aware routing to avoid cold-start delays and memory overloads. At the global level, it enforces workflow-aware prioritization to minimize head-of-line blocking for interactive tasks. Across prototype experiments and trace-driven simulations, Maestro reduces KV-reservation HBM by 67.2% and improves high-contention SLO attainment over EDF by 23.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [227] [JiRAIYA: A Reputation-Based Hierarchical Federated Learning Framework on Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13180)
*Venkata Raghava Kurada,Pallav Kumar Baruah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合Web3技术的联邦学习框架，通过分层架构和共识机制解决了传统FL系统透明度和可审计性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统主要部署在企业环境中，存在透明度有限和可审计性受限的问题。聚合过程不透明，难以确定哪些模型更新被接受或丢弃，而当前的缓解策略通常依赖外部验证器，引入了额外的计算和通信开销。

Method: 提出了一个利用现有Web3技术的新颖FL框架，采用分层架构，由委托管理者在其各自的联盟内协调FL训练过程。使用新颖性检测和共识机制的组合来减轻对抗性和投毒攻击。模型更新被编码并广播给所有管理者，管理者独立评估其有效性，经共识批准的模型更新被纳入全局模型。此外，还采用了基于声誉分数的备份机制来确保模型生成。

Result: 在真实世界场景下进行的广泛实验证明了所提框架的有效性和韧性，突显了其在传统企业环境之外实现透明FL的潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功利用Web3技术增强了FL训练过程中的透明度、信任度和可审计性，为超越传统企业环境的透明FL部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning(FL) is predominantly deployed in enterprise environments, where limited transparency and restricted auditability hinder broader adoption. Existing FL systems often suffer from opaque aggregation processes, making it unclear which model updates are accepted or discarded. Current mitigation strategies typically rely on external validators introducing additional computational and communication overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel FL framework that leverages existing Web3 technologies to enhance transparency, trust and auditability throughout the training process. The framework adopts a hierarchical architecture in which delegated managers orchestrate the FL training process within their respective federations. To mitigate adversarial and poisoning attacks, a combination of novelty detection and consensus mechanisms were employed. Model updates are encoded and broad casted to all managers, who independently evaluate their validity and those model updates that are approved by the consensus are incorporated into the global model. Additionally, a reputation score based backup mechanism is employed to ensure model generation. Extensive experiments conducted under real world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness, resilience of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential to enable transparent FL beyond traditional enterprise setting.

</details>


### [228] [Dynamic Resource Management in Production HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13266)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Guillaume Houzeaux,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个实用的MPI可塑性方法论，通过DMR框架实现，无需侵入式代码修改即可在生产HPC系统中实现动态资源管理，在保持性能的同时显著减少了节点小时消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模科学应用通常表现出时变行为特性，但生产HPC集群仍然依赖僵化的固定规模资源分配，大多数动态技术仅停留在实验室原型阶段。现有系统缺乏实用的动态资源管理方案，阻碍了动态资源管理在HPC领域的应用推广。

Method: 提出了一个实用的MPI可塑性（malleability）方法论，通过动态资源管理（DMR）框架实现。该方法与现有高性能计算软件栈和运维实践集成，采用非侵入式设计，无需对应用程序进行侵入式代码修改或调度器修改即可集成。将DMR API集成到两个大规模科学应用中，在三台TOP500超级计算机上以真实生产配置进行部署评估。

Result: 非侵入式可塑性解决方案在受控环境中达到了与静态基线相当的性能，同时对于相同工作负载显著减少了节点小时消耗。该方法证明可以在使用原生资源管理器的生产系统上有效利用可塑性特性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可塑性可以在生产系统上使用原生资源管理器得到有效利用，降低了动态资源管理在HPC领域采用的门槛，为动态资源管理在生产环境中的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Many large-scale scientific applications exhibit time-varying behavior, yet production HPC clusters still rely on rigid, fixed-size allocations, and most dynamic techniques remain confined to laboratory prototypes. This work presents a practical MPI malleability methodology that integrates with state-of-the-art high-performance computing (HPC) software stacks and operational practices. The methodology is implemented in the Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) framework and is designed to ease adoption by existing applications without requiring intrusive code changes or scheduler modifications. We evaluate our approach by integrating the DMR API into two large-scale scientific applications and deploying them on three TOP500 supercomputers under realistic production configurations. Our non-invasive malleability solution achieves performance comparable to static baselines in controlled environments while substantially reducing node-hour consumption for identical workloads. These results show that malleability can be effectively exploited on production systems using vanilla resource managers, lowering the barrier to adoption of dynamic resource management in HPC.

</details>


### [229] [Skiplists with Foresight: Skipping Cache Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13321)
*Tomer Cory,Niv Sulimany,Erez Petrank*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Foresight，一种缓存友好的跳表优化方法，在微基准测试中实现高达45%的吞吐量提升，在DBx1000内存数据库中实现高达15%的端到端性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳表是一种广泛用于系统和应用中索引数据存储的基础数据结构，但传统跳表在缓存性能方面存在优化空间。当前缺乏一种易于集成且高效的缓存友好优化方法。

Method: 提出Foresight，一种针对跳表的缓存友好优化方法。该方法是一种精准优化，易于集成到各种跳表设计中。针对并发场景下的显著同步挑战进行了识别和解决。

Result: 在一个顺序跳表和三个并发跳表设计中应用Foresight，在微基准测试中观察到高达45%的吞吐量提升。在DBx1000内存数据库中应用于基于跳表的索引时，获得高达15%的端到端性能提升。

Conclusion: Foresight是一种有效的跳表优化方法，具有易于集成的特点，能够显著提升跳表的缓存性能，在多种场景下都取得了良好的性能提升效果。

Abstract: A skiplist is a fundamental data structure widely used in systems and applications for indexing data stores. In this work, we introduce Foresight, a cache-friendly skiplist optimization. Extending Foresight to concurrent settings introduces significant synchronization challenges that we identify and address. Foresight is a surgical optimization, easy to integrate into a wide variety of skiplist designs. We apply it to one sequential and three concurrent skiplist designs and observe throughput improvements of up to 45% in microbenchmarks. When applied to a skiplist-based index in the DBx1000 in-memory database, Foresight yields end-to-end performance gains of up to 15%.

</details>


### [230] [Work Stealing for the 2D-Mesh Topology of Satellite Constellations in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13329)
*Mia Reitz,Dorian Chenet,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对空间边缘计算(SEC)稀疏网络拓扑的特点，提出了一种邻居优先的任务窃取策略，在避免多跳通信的同时保持了与全局窃取策略相当的负载均衡性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步多任务(AMT)运行时假设网络为全连接且延迟低且均匀，采用全局随机窃取策略。然而，低地球轨道(LEO)卫星星座形成的空间边缘计算集群具有稀疏网状拓扑，卫星间通信需要多跳中继，导致延迟累积。这种拓扑结构使得传统的全局窃取策略效率降低，需要针对SEC环境重新设计任务窃取策略。

Method: 提出邻居优先(neighbor-only)任务窃取策略，限制Worker仅从直接相连的邻居节点窃取任务，从而避免多跳通信带来的延迟。同时建立了分析模型来评估该策略在不同星座规模下的延迟优势，并在HPC集群上通过模拟网状拓扑和均匀低延迟链路进行了实验验证，隔离了受害者选择策略的影响。

Result: 分析模型表明，限制窃取范围带来的单次尝试延迟优势随星座规模增大而增长。初步实验结果显示，邻居优先策略在平衡和非规则工作负载下的性能与全局窃取策略相比差距仅为约2.2%，说明限制受害者集合在当前实验环境下不会损害负载均衡能力。

Conclusion: 邻居优先窃取策略在小规模测试中能够达到与全局窃取策略相当的性能，而分析模型表明在更大规模的星座部署中，邻居优先策略将变得更具优势。这为将AMT编程模型适配到空间边缘计算环境提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) is a parallel programming model used in High Performance Computing (HPC). An AMT runtime can distribute fine-grained tasks across processing units called workers, through work stealing: when a worker has no tasks left to process, it tries to steal tasks from other workers. Workers are not restricted to a single compute node but can also be distributed across multiple nodes of an HPC cluster. Existing AMT runtimes assume a fully connected network with low, uniform latency and perform global work stealing, selecting another worker at random from all workers in the system.
  Space Edge Computing (SEC) uses constellations of satellites in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) as distributed compute clusters. Unlike HPC clusters, LEO satellites communicate through inter-satellite links that form a sparse mesh topology. Reaching a distant satellite requires multiple hops, each adding latency.
  As a step toward adapting AMT to SEC, this paper proposes a neighbor-only work stealing strategy in which workers steal exclusively from directly connected neighbors, avoiding multi-hop communication. An analytical model shows that restricting stealing this way yields a per-attempt latency advantage that grows with constellation size. Preliminary experiments on an HPC cluster with an emulated mesh over uniform low-latency links isolate the effect of victim selection: the neighbor-only strategy performs within ~2.2% of global stealing on both balanced and irregular workloads, indicating that restricting the victim set does not harm load balancing in this setting. Taken together, the experiments suggest that neighbor-only stealing can be on a par with global stealing, and the model suggests that neighbor-only stealing becomes preferable at scale.

</details>


### [231] [Temporal Conductance and Bounds on the Voter Model for Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13374)
*Tatiana Rocha Avila,Holger Dell,John Lapinskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了时间传导率（temporal conductance）这一新概念，用于分析动态网络上的选民模型，证明了其共识时间的上界为O(m/(d_min·Φ))且该界紧确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于静态传导率分析动态网络选民模型的方法存在局限性：当网络某个时刻的快照不连通时，静态传导率会坍缩为0，无法有效捕捉动态网络通过不同时刻出现的边所形成的连通性。因此需要一种更通用的连通性度量方法。

Method: 作者引入了时间传导率Φ这一新概念，与静态传导率不同，它能捕捉在不同时刻出现的边所形成的网络连通性。基于此概念，将Berenbrink等人关于静态网络上选民模型的理论结果推广到动态网络场景。

Result: 证明了在具有m条边、最小度d_min和时间传导率Φ的动态网络上，标准选民模型达到共识的期望时间至多为O(m/(d_min·Φ))。进一步证明该上界在常数因子范围内是紧确的（tight）。

Conclusion: 时间传导率为分析时序网络上的动力学过程提供了一个有用的原语，该概念有望推广应用于其他时序网络动力学分析，甚至更一般的非齐次马尔可夫链研究。

Abstract: The voter model is a classical stochastic process that models how opinions might spread through a network: at each step, every node lazily adopts the opinion of a random neighbour; eventually all nodes share the same opinion (consensus). Stronger connectivity should yield faster consensus. Berenbrink, Giakkoupis, Kermarrec, and Mallmann-Trenn (ICALP 2016) make this precise via the network's conductance: if the network has $m$ edges, minimum degree $d_{\min}$, and conductance at least $φ$, then the voter model reaches consensus in expected $O(m/(d_{\min}φ))$ steps. Their results extend to dynamic networks with fixed vertex degrees by considering the network's conductance at each time step.
  We introduce temporal conductance $Φ$, a more general connectivity measure for dynamic networks. Unlike static conductance, which collapses to $0$ whenever some snapshot is disconnected, $Φ$ captures connectivity through edges that appear at different times. We generalise the results of Berenbrink et al. from static conductance to temporal conductance, showing that the expected consensus time of the standard voter model is at most $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is tight up to constant factors. We expect temporal conductance to be a useful primitive for analysing other dynamics on temporal networks, and potentially time-inhomogeneous Markov chains more generally.

</details>


### [232] [GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13501)
*Xinwei Qiang,Yifan Hu,Shixuan Sun,Jing Yang,Han Zhao,Chen Chen,Yu Feng,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出GF-DiT，一种用于Diffusion Transformers（DiT）服务的弹性运行时系统，通过将GPU并行度作为可调度资源动态调整，显著提升了图像和视频生成的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DiT服务系统为每个请求分配固定的并行配置，无法适应请求间、执行阶段和系统条件的显著异构性，导致GPU利用率低下和服务质量下降。作者认为应当将GPU并行度视为一等可调度资源。

Method: 提出GF-DiT系统，引入异步执行抽象将请求分解为独立可调度的轨迹任务，实现在线GPU重分配；同时提出无组集合通信（group-free collectives），一种轻量级通信抽象，支持任意执行组的低开销在线形成和重配置。

Result: 在代表性图像和视频扩散工作负载上评估，相比静态并行配置，GF-DiT吞吐量提升最高6.01倍，平均延迟降低最高95%，SLO违规率降低最高90%，通信组设置开销从778毫秒降至约60微秒。

Conclusion: 通过将GPU并行度作为可调度资源并实现弹性并行机制，GF-DiT有效解决了DiT工作负载的异构性挑战，大幅提升了系统性能和资源利用率，为高效DiT服务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality.
  This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups.
  We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.

</details>


### [233] [Finding Conservation Laws of Large Dynamical Systems with Tasks and Futures: A Case Study in Utilizing Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13623)
*Rüdiger Nather*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出await_delete构造扩展future语义以支持安全内存重用，并基于此设计了稠密对称矩阵分块求逆算法，在大规模问题上实现近似线性强扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Future模型虽能处理细粒度数据依赖，但其值不可变性限制了原位内存更新，而内存复用是高性能线性代数的关键需求。

Method: 引入await_delete构造扩展传统future语义，允许消费者完成后安全地复用值；在此基础上提出基于future的稠密对称矩阵分块求逆算法，并在Taskflow扩展版中实现。

Result: 强扩展实验表明：小规模问题存在显著开销，但大规模矩阵上实现近似线性加速；通过分摊阈值分析验证future在大规模线性代数中的可行性。

Conclusion: 结合await_delete扩展的future模型可有效支持原位更新，成为大规模线性代数计算的高性能工具。

Abstract: As parallel workloads grow in complexity, managing fine-grained data dependencies becomes a critical challenge. Futures offer a promising model for handling these dependencies, particularly in irregular algorithms, but they also come with the restriction of value-immutability. This immutability limits the ability to perform in-place memory updates, a necessity for high-performance linear algebra where memory recycling is paramount.
  In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a new construct, await_delete, which extends traditional future semantics to allow safe value reuse once consumers are finished. Building on this extension, we present a novel future-based algorithm for the block-wise inversion of dense, symmetric matrices, motivated by a recent algorithm for finding conservation laws of dynamical systems.
  We implement our approach in an extended version of Taskflow and evaluate it through strong-scaling experiments. Our results demonstrate that while futures incur significant overhead on smaller problem sizes, they achieve nearly linear scaling on large matrices. We analyze the amortization threshold and show that futures are a viable high-performance tool for large-scale linear algebra.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [SAIGuard: Communication-State Simulation for Proactive Defense of LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12474)
*Ruxue Shi,Yili Wang,Mengnan Du,Qinggang Zhang,Rui Miao,Yixin Liu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出SAIGuard，一种用于大语言模型多智能体系统的主动防御框架，通过通信状态模拟和消息净化/重生成，在恶意消息传播前进行拦截，优于传统的反应式防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型多智能体系统（MAS）防御主要采用反应式范式，即在执行后检测并隔离有害智能体，这可能导致不可逆的损害并降低协作效用。由于MAS的通信驱动特性，安全风险可能在智能体间传播并引发系统级故障，因此需要一种主动防御机制。

Method: 提出Simulation-aware Interception Guard (SAIGuard)框架：(1)在MAS交互图上执行通信状态模拟；(2)评估传入消息对本地智能体状态和全局MAS状态的影响；(3)通过重构偏差从良性通信模式中检测危险消息；(4)对可疑消息进行净化或重生成，而非隔离智能体，阻止其在系统中传播。

Result: 在多种拓扑结构和攻击场景下的实验表明，SAIGuard能够降低攻击成功率，同时保持MAS的实用性，性能优于反应式防御方法。

Conclusion: SAIGuard作为主动防御框架，能够在恶意消息传播到系统之前进行拦截和处理，有效平衡了安全性与系统协作效用，为MAS安全提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) solve complex tasks through inter-agent collaboration, but their communication-driven nature also allows security risks to spread across agents and trigger system-wide failures. Existing MAS defenses mainly follow a reactive paradigm after execution by detecting and isolating harmful agents, which may cause irreversible damage and degrade collaborative utility. To address this, we propose a proactive defense framework for MAS security, namely a Simulation-aware Interception Guard (SAIGuard). SAIGuard performs communication-state simulation over the MAS interaction graph, estimates the impact of incoming messages on local agent states and the global MAS state, and detects risky messages via reconstruction deviations from benign communication patterns. Instead of isolating agents, SAIGuard sanitizes or regenerates suspicious messages before it propagation into system. Experiments across diverse topologies and attack scenarios show that SAIGuard reduces attack success rates while maintaining MAS utility, outperforming reactive defenses.

</details>


### [235] [Smarter Saboteurs, Better Fixers: Scaling & Security in Linear Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12709)
*Timothy McAllister,Sina Abdidizaji,Ivan Garibay,Ozlem Ozmen Garibay*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文发现大模型更易执行恶意指令，但通过添加轻量级修复阶段可使线性多智能体工作流恢复弹性，证明线性结构在对抗攻击下可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）被广泛部署，其协作结构对抗恶意攻击的韧性成为关键安全问题。攻击者可能利用提示注入或越狱技术破坏MAS工作流中的个体智能体，但模型规模与系统级韧性之间的相互作用机制尚不明确。本文旨在研究模型规模如何影响线性多智能体工作流的安全性。

Method: 在HumanEval基准测试上，对两个开源模型家族在不同规模下的表现进行实验，分析模型规模对线性多智能体工作流安全性的影响。研究重点考察控制场景与恶意指令场景下的性能差异，并提出通过附加轻量级终端修复阶段（Fixer stage）来增强系统韧性。

Result: 实验揭示了合规-修正对称性：较大规模模型更倾向于忠实执行恶意指令，在未经修正的工作流中，控制到恶意场景的性能下降在27B模型上达到53.7个百分点。然而，通过附加轻量级终端修复阶段，该性能下降坍缩至0.6个百分点，并与控制级别性能恢复统计平价。这证明严格线性协作结构在规模足够大时对对抗攻击具有可行性和韧性。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了之前认为线性拓扑结构本质脆弱的观点，表明这种脆弱性可能源于缺乏修正机制而非线性结构本身。通过在终端添加修正阶段，严格线性协作结构在大规模模型下能够实现对抗韧性，为构建安全可靠的多智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: As LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) are deployed in the wild, the resilience of their collaboration structures against adversarial compromise becomes a critical safety concern. Attackers may leverage prompt-injection or jailbreaking to sabotage individual agents within MAS workflows, but the interaction between model scaling and system-level resilience remains poorly understood. This paper investigates how model scale affects the security of linear multi-agent workflows. Our experiments across scales of two open-weight model families on the HumanEval benchmark reveal a compliance-correction symmetry: larger models are far more likely to faithfully execute malicious instructions, with the control-to-malicious performance drop reaching 53.7pp at 27B in uncorrected pipelines. However, appending a lightweight terminal Fixer stage collapses this to 0.6pp and restores statistical parity with control-level performance, demonstrating that strictly linear collaboration structures can be viable and resilient to adversaries at this scale, and suggesting that the brittleness previously attributed to linear topology may stem from a lack of correction.

</details>


### [236] [The Internet of Agentic AI: Communication, Coordination, and Collective Intelligence at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12835)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了智联网的愿景，这是一个开放生态系统，异构AI智能体可以相互发现、协商、交换上下文、调用工具并执行工作流。论文综合了多学科基础，系统分析了大规模智能体生态系统所需的核心架构和机制，并指出了六大核心研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI智能体的快速涌现，人工智能正在从孤立的模型推理转变为分布式的推理、通信和行动系统。当前缺乏一个统一的框架来描述和管理这种跨云、边缘、设备、组织和网络物理环境的异构智能体生态系统，因此需要建立IoAI的愿景和理论基础。

Method: 综合运用单智能体代理AI、多智能体系统、分布式计算、通信网络、博弈论和安全工程等多个学科的理论基础，系统性研究智能体部署模型、工作流生命周期、通信协议、互操作性层、资源管理挑战和信任架构，并通过自适应制造和分布式操作协调案例进行验证。

Result: 建立了一个全面的IoAI框架，系统分析了可扩展智能体生态系统所需的架构和机制，明确了六大核心研究挑战：受控涌现、语义互操作性、安全身份、激励相容协调、资源感知编排和大规模自主智能体网络治理。

Conclusion: IoAI代表了人工智能发展的新范式，需要跨学科协作解决智能体发现、协调、安全、资源管理和治理等核心问题，为构建大规模、开放、可信的自主智能体网络生态系统奠定了理论基础和研究方向。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of autonomous AI agents is transforming artificial intelligence from isolated model inference into distributed systems of reasoning, communication, and action. This paper develops the vision of the Internet of Agentic AI (IoAI): an open ecosystem in which heterogeneous agents discover one another, negotiate responsibilities, exchange context, invoke tools, and execute workflows across cloud, edge, device, organizational, and cyber-physical environments. We synthesize foundations from single-agent agentic AI, multi-agent systems, distributed computing, communication networks, game theory, and security engineering to characterize the architectures and mechanisms required for scalable agent ecosystems. The paper examines agent deployment models, workflow lifecycles, communication protocols, interoperability layers, resource-management challenges, and trust architectures, with case studies in adaptive manufacturing and distributed operational coordination. The resulting framework highlights the central research challenges of controlled emergence, semantic interoperability, secure identity, incentive-compatible coordination, resource-aware orchestration, and governance for large-scale networks of autonomous agents.

</details>


### [237] [Effects of Social Interactions in Self-Organising Railway Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13068)
*Fabio Oddi,Federico Naldini,Leo D'Amato,Grégory Marlière,Paola Pellegrini,Vito Trianni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 在自组织交通管理系统中，研究预测邻域视界的结构参数影响，发现短时间视界足以满足需求，长时间视界反而损害局部可处理性和计算响应性且无全局优化增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自组织交通管理作为解决复杂现实网络扩展性的方案，需要列车预测邻域、生成交通计划假设并通过共识机制与邻居达成一致。预测邻域视界作为关键设计变量，直接决定社会交互图的大小和密度，其对局部子问题复杂性和分布式共识动态的影响需要探索其权衡关系。

Method: 采用闭环仿真框架评估视界变化对整个去中心化协调过程的影响，从初始冲突检测到分布式调度共识，分析视界选择带来的潜在权衡：平衡局部可处理性和计算响应性与全局调度一致性和安全关键环境可行性之间的关系。

Result: 与直觉相反，实证结果表明短时间视界已足够满足需求，而长时间视界会损害局部可处理性和计算响应性，且在全球调度最优性方面没有任何增益。

Conclusion: 预测邻域视界的选择对自组织交通管理系统性能具有重要影响，研究发现较短的时间视界能够在保证系统性能的同时提高计算效率，为去中心化交通协调系统的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Recent research is exploring self-organised traffic management as a solution for scaling to complex real-world networks. In such a system, trains predict their neighbourhood, produce traffic plan hypotheses, and agree via consensus with neighbours on a future traffic plan to be implemented. This paper investigates a structural parameter within this pipeline: the predictive neighbourhood horizon. The horizon is used by trains to identify future potential conflicts with neighbours, and to establish the local interaction topology, that is, the subset of trains to negotiate with. As the primary design variable, the horizon directly determines the size and density of the social interaction graph, whereas its impact on the complexity of local sub-problems and the distributed consensus dynamics represents a trade-off to be explored. Through a closed-loop simulation framework the study evaluates how variations of the horizon impact the overall decentralised coordination process, from initial conflict detection to distributed schedule consensus. The analysis focuses on investigating the potential trade-off introduced by the horizon choice: balancing local tractability and computational responsiveness with the need for global schedule coherence and feasibility in safety-critical environments. Contrary to intuition, our empirical results indicate that the short time horizons suffice, while long values compromise local tractability and computational responsiveness with no gain in global schedule optimality.

</details>


### [238] [$α$-fair heterogeneous agent reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13076)
*Yao-hua Franck Xu,Tayeb Lemlouma,Jean-Marie Bonnin,Arnaud Braud*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出α-公平HATRL框架，通过公平优势函数动态调整智能体效用权重，在保证单调改进和纳什均衡收敛的理论保证下，实现了比纯功利主义方法更优的社会效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体协作优化通常采用功利主义目标最大化整体效率，但忽视奖励分配公平性，导致不公平的"领导-跟随"动态。现有公平性方法（包括奖励塑形）破坏马尔可夫博弈的平稳性或缺乏严格理论保证，在公平目标方法与理论安全学习框架之间存在关键差距。

Method: 提出连接α-公平性与异构智能体信赖域学习（HATRL）的新框架，利用公平优势函数基于期望回报动态加权智能体效用，使全局目标通过参数α从纯功利主义效率过渡到α-公平性福利，确保单调改进和向纳什均衡收敛。提出两种实用算法：α-公平HATRPO和α-公平HAPPO。

Result: 在CleanUp和CommonHarvest等序贯社会困境实验中，新算法从功利主义角度表现优于HATRL算法，同时实现更高的社会效益结果。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥合了公平目标方法与理论安全学习框架之间的差距，证明了在保持理论保证的前提下，公平性考虑不仅不损害反而能提升多智能体协作的整体性能。

Abstract: Cooperation in multi-agent systems is typically optimized through utilitarian objectives that maximize overall efficiency but fail to account for reward distribution, often resulting in inequitable "leader-follower" dynamics. While fairness-based approaches encourage pro-social behaviors where every agent benefits from cooperation, many current algorithms - including those utilizing reward shaping - break the stationarity of Markov Games or lack rigorous theoretical guarantees. This creates a critical gap between fair objective methods and theoretically safe learning frameworks. We propose a novel framework that bridges $α$-fairness with Heterogeneous-Agent Trust Region Learning (HATRL), ensuring monotonic improvement and convergence toward Nash Equilibria. Our approach leverages a fair advantage function that dynamically weights agent utilities based on their expected returns, allowing the global objective to transition from purely utilitarian efficiency to $α$-fairness welfare based on the parameter $α$. We introduce two practical algorithms, $α$-fair HATRPO and $α$-fair HAPPO, and demonstrate through experiments in sequential social dilemmas like CleanUp and CommonHarvest that they perform better than HATRL's algorithms from a utilitarian point of view while achieving socially higher outcomes.

</details>


### [239] [See What I See, Know What I Think: Dense Latent Communication Across Heterogeneous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13594)
*Siyi Chen,Xiaoyan Zhang,Meng Wu,Jonathan Tremblay,Valts Blukis,Stan Birchfield,Rene Vidal,Alvaro Velasquez,Sijia Liu,Qing Qu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种密集对齐方法，用于异构多智能体系统间的KV缓存通信。该方法通过轻量级跨模型缓存变换和两阶段训练（重建+生成），在6个基准测试上超越现有异构基线，计算成本仅为文本通信的1/2到1/3，并在上下文无关场景中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构多智能体通信主要依赖文本，存在解码-编码过程的高损耗和高成本问题；而KV缓存通信方法多局限于同构模型，或假设共享输入且仅用于引导。核心挑战在于：异构智能体间能否实现足够好的对齐，以传递"所见"和"所思"？

Method: 提出密集对齐方法：(1)信息结构分析揭示上下文感知转移依赖稀疏推理信号，上下文无关转移需要密集上下文知识保存；(2)轻量级跨模型缓存变换模块；(3)两阶段训练：先重建后生成。在{Qwen3-4B, 8B, 14B}三个模型间六个方向进行实验。

Result: 在六个领域内/外基准测试中：(1)全面超越现有异构基线；(2)上下文感知场景下性能匹配或超越文本通信，计算成本降低约2-3倍；(3)上下文无关转移场景中，先前方法失效而本方法保持有效。

Conclusion: 证明了异构智能体间通过KV缓存进行"思维读取"式通信的可行性。密集对齐方法成功解决了跨模型潜在对齐的核心挑战，为异构多智能体系统提供了一种高效、低成本的通信范式，显著扩展了KV缓存通信的应用边界。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems communicate mostly through text, paying a lossy and expensive decode and re-encode cost. KV-cache communication is a promising alternative, yet most prior work is homogeneous, using duplicate copies of the same model, and avoids the central challenge of cross-model latent alignment; existing heterogeneous methods are also restrictive, typically assuming shared input and using transferred caches mainly for steering. We study a more fundamental question: can heterogeneous agents be aligned well enough to perform real "mind reading" and transfer both what one agent sees and how it thinks? Our information-structure analysis reveals a duality: context-aware transfer is driven by sparse reasoning signals, while context-unaware transfer, where the receiver sees no input, requires dense contextual knowledge preservation. Motivated by this, we propose dense alignment for heterogeneous KV-cache communication via a lightweight cross-model cache transformation and two-phase training: reconstruction followed by generation. Across all six directions of {Qwen3-4B, 8B, 14B} and six in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, our method outperforms prior heterogeneous baselines, matches or exceeds text communication in context-aware settings at roughly 2 to 3 times lower compute, and remains effective in context-unaware transfer where prior methods collapse.

</details>


### [240] [Tuning Agent-Based Predator-Prey Models Toward Lotka-Volterra Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13639)
*Corinna Mandl,Siddharth Chaturvedi,Marcel van Gerven*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过优化环境和人口参数，使基于大规模智能体的捕食者-猎物系统产生类似经典Lotka-Volterra循环的种群动态，并在ABMax框架中实现了高效仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模智能体模型（ABM）在模拟复杂适应系统时面临核心挑战：模型包含大量局部规则和参数，微小变化可能导致系统行为失控、种群崩溃或在人工边界处饱和，这使得参数调优极其困难。

Method: 构建了一个连续捕食者-猎物系统，其中绵羊和狼为具有局部感知、内部能量和基于循环神经网络控制器的主动智能体；设计了基于特征的损失函数，奖励持续振荡、相位滞后、有界种群和长期持续性；在ABMax（基于JAX的智能体建模框架）上实现，支持硬件加速器上的高效批量仿真；分两阶段优化参数：先针对随机控制器，再针对演化控制器。

Result: 成功优化了环境和人口参数，使系统产生的种群动态类似于经典Lotka-Volterra循环；验证了基于特征的损失函数在引导参数调优方面的有效性；展示了ABMax框架在处理大规模智能体模型时的计算优势。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的损失函数和优化策略，可以有效调节复杂智能体模型的参数，使其产生理想的种群动态行为；硬件加速的仿真框架为大规模智能体模型的研究提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Recent growth in compute power has made it increasingly feasible to use large-scale agent-based models to simulate complex adaptive systems. A central difficulty is that such models contain many local rules and parameters, where small changes can lead to runaway behaviour, population collapse, or saturation at artificial bounds. We study this problem in a continuous predator-prey system where sheep and wolves are active agents with local sensing, internal energy, and recurrent neural network-based controllers. We ask whether environmental and demographic parameters can be tuned so that the resulting population dynamics resemble classical Lotka-Volterra cycles. We optimise these parameters with a feature-based loss that rewards sustained oscillations, phase lag, bounded populations, and long-term persistence, first for random controllers and then for evolved controllers in a more naturalistic setting. The model is implemented in ABMax, a JAX-based agent-based modelling framework that enables efficient batched simulation on hardware accelerators.

</details>
